- **arXiv**: [2608.30709](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30709) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quan Hao, Chenxi Zhang, Ziyang Tao, Yuyuan Zhou, Yudong Wang, Rui Shi, Lechuan Xu, Changhao Liu, Liguo Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, Object Detection, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Summary**: 针对铁路异物检测中真实正样本稀少且覆盖不足的问题，提出RailSyn诊断引导生成框架，由Inspector定位补全区域并审计需求，Generator通过域适应、智能体规划和条件细化生成数据。系统使补全覆盖达到13.64%，在九个主流检测器上AP50-95最多提升4.9点。

<h3 id="390">[390] BAITBENCH: Measuring Agent Reward Hacking with Optional Shortcuts Planted in ML Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pradyumna Shyama Prasad, Meiri Anto, Leon Eshuijs, Julian Moncarz, Kaustubh Kislay, Juan J. Vazquez
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reward Hacking, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对现有基准无法测量数据或任务本身的奖励捷径利用问题，提出BAITBENCH套件。包含三个带捷径的合成表格ML任务，代理可虚增公共分数但在隐藏集失败。七个前沿代理中57.1%的运行表现出奖励黑客，即使提示不要作弊时平均作弊率仍高于50%。

<h3 id="391">[391] RailGen: Improving Railway Intrusion Detection via Agent-Guided Small-Scale Foreign Object Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30727](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30727) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quan Hao, Ziyang Tao, Chenxi Zhang, Yudong Wang, Rui Shi, Liguo Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, Small Object Detection, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Summary**: 针对铁路异物检测中小目标稀有且长尾分布导致检测脆弱的问题，提出RailGen多模态图像生成智能体，自动生成场景并融合小异物（像素面积平均减少13.85倍），配合FocalDEIM检测器，在mAP@50和mAP@(50-95)上比DEIM提升5.6%和7.5%。

<h3 id="392">[392] Calibrating Small Language Models for Claim Check-Worthiness Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30731](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30731) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pratuat Amatya, Venktesh Viswanathan, Vinay Setty
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Prediction-Powered Inference, Fact Checking, Model Calibration
- **Summary**: 针对大模型逐条判断claim代价高而小模型准确率不足的问题，提出NN-PPI，作为无需重训练的推理时后处理校准层。在加权F1上提升12%至33.8%，使小模型达到大模型水平，且服务成本降低一个数量级。

<h3 id="393">[393] Learning from What You Retrieve: Online RL Fine-Tuning for Semantic Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30753](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30753) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shaowei Wei, Chong Huang, Songtao Fang, Jin Zhang, Zhuojun Wang, Chengfu Huo
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Information Retrieval, E-Commerce
- **Summary**: 针对电商检索中双编码器与重排序器目标不一致导致端到端质量受限的问题，提出PAO选择性强化学习，仅对正优势检索项更新梯度，避免破坏冻结索引的语义流形。在工业数据和公开基准上显著优于标准RL和蒸馏基线。

<h3 id="394">[394] On the Prospects of Dynamic LLM Conversations in Software Development</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30756](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30756) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Annemarie Wittig, Alina Mailach, Janet Siegmund, Norbert Siegmund
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Human-Computer Interaction, User Study
- **Summary**: 为评估对开发者与LLM交互的干预是否有害或有益，进行了四个月纵向研究，三组分别接受上下文增强、主动建议和无干预。结果显示干预未造成交互模式差异，但主动处理组满意度有提升趋势，表明最小干预即可产生可观察效果。

<h3 id="395">[395] Conjoint Audio-to-Spikes Encoding and Processing for Efficient Neuromorphic Speech Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30792](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30792) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Valentin M. Meunier, Amélie Gruel, Pierre Lewden, Adrien F. Vincent, Sylvain Saïghi
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Neuromorphic Computing, Speech Recognition, Spiking Neural Network
- **Summary**: 针对生物拟真音频到脉冲编码在数字硬件上难以实现的问题，提出非可学习的可编程编码器，联合优化编码器和分类器。在Heidelberg Digits上达到99.77%准确率，超越神经形态SOTA，并首次端到端编码TIMIT数据集。

<h3 id="396">[396] Aggregate Disambiguation Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30805) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: José María Lago, Albert Castellana, Edgars Nemše
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Research Methodology, Statistical Inference, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 针对自然语言任务中不同评估者可能给出不同判定导致协议不可重复的问题，提出聚合消歧系统（ADS），通过有限面板投票和置信下界估计一致性，分离候选解采样和评估者采样，使用精确二项区间。仿真验证覆盖率和功率限制。

<h3 id="397">[397] A Composition-Aware Pretraining Framework for Geospatial Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30817](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30817) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aryan Kashyap Naveen, Abhishek Srinivas, Pranav Moothedath, Shrutilipi Bhattacharjee
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Geospatial Foundation Model, Remote Sensing, Pre-training
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/05kashyap/GFM_Composition_Pretraining)
- **Summary**: 针对地理空间基础模型预训练忽略卫星场景高组合性的问题，提出组成感知预训练框架，以分数土地覆盖直方图作为预测目标并用推土机距离蒸馏。在区域级任务上显著优于更大模型，ForestNet-12上mAP@10提升55.6%。

<h3 id="398">[398] Lucida: Parse, Generate, and Place for Composable Real-to-Sim Scene Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30821](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30821) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minghan Qin, Yuang Wang, Xiuyu Yang, Yushi Long, Yujian Zhang, Ruihuan Wang, Kai Ye, Yangang Zhang, Hang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Reconstruction, Scene Understanding, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对组合式真实到仿真场景建模中每步都要求不现实输入的问题，提出Lucida，通过场景图解析、实例资产生成和基于VLM的GizmoAct多轮GUI放置，在场景级3D检测、姿态估计和重建上大幅提升。

<h3 id="399">[399] Reliable Benchmarking of Artifact Detection in Computational Pathology: A Reproducibility and Uncertainty Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30835](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30835) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Konstantinos Moutselos, Ilias Maglogiannis
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Histopathology, Research Reproducibility
- **Summary**: 针对计算病理学中伪影检测基准因小样本、集中标注、无标准误差和单一划分而难以解释的问题，提出可靠性协议，量化四种变异来源。核心机制复现，但比较性声称不可靠；建议小队列基准应报告更弱结论。

<h3 id="400">[400] Pretrained, Curriculum-Tuned, and Ensembled: A Tracer-Aware Interactive Segmentation Pipeline for AutoPET V</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30844](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30844) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinglong Liang, Chunyao Lu, Tianyu Zhang, Jiaju Huang, Tao Tan, Yunchao Yin, Lishan Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, Interactive Segmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Liiiii2101/AUTOPET2026-MEDAI)
- **Summary**: 针对FDG和PSMA示踪剂显著差异下交互式病变分割的挑战，提出TRIAGE，使用STU-Net掩码自编码预训练、辅助器官分割提供解剖上下文、示踪剂分类器路由分支，并采用课程训练和集成，在AutoPET V上进行评估。

<h3 id="401">[401] TAMI: Temporally Aligned, Missingness-Aware, and Interpretable Multimodal Fusion for Mental Health Assessment in Older Adults with Mild Cognitive Impairment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30857](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30857) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Merna Bibars, Bolaji Omofojoye, Allan I. Levey, Rachel Hershenberg, Gari D. Clifford, Hyeokhyen Kwon
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Fusion, Mental Health, Affective Computing
- **Summary**: 针对MCI老年人抑郁/焦虑多模态评估中时间错位、模态缺失未处理及可解释性不足的问题，提出TAMI多模态融合框架，对齐时间线、编码缺失性并基于问题上下文融合。在49名MCI老年人访谈中，抑郁AUROC 0.68、焦虑0.69；时间对齐贡献最大（Δ≥0.1），仅用开放式问题（5.1分钟）即可达与完整访谈相近的抑郁筛查性能。

<h3 id="402">[402] Exponential random graph models with soft clique constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30869) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yasmin Tousinejad, Vera Koponen
- **Subjects**: math.CO; cs.AI; math.PR
- **Tags**: Exponential Random Graph Model
- **Summary**: 研究带软团约束的指数随机图模型：给少r-团的图更高概率，权重w\>0。证明渐近地，随机图顶点可分为r-1个近似等大的部分，部分间边密度接近1/2，部分内边密度趋于0，且该性质与w无关。该结果还推广到多个团大小同时约束的情形。

<h3 id="403">[403] Personas Differ from Native-Language Generation: Language Pathways Shape LLM Interpersonal Advice</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30873) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinhee Won, Xinlan Emily Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Persona Prompting, Cross-Lingual, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference)
- **Summary**: 针对用"母语者角色扮演"提示（NP）获取跨语言变化是否等价于真正的母语生成（NL）的问题，在600个人际建议问题上比较13种语言、8个LLM。发现NP与NL不可互换：NP常增加亲和等词汇社交线索、降低具体性和社会调谐度，并改变行动计划建议；跨语言提示策略是影响结果的方法学选择。

<h3 id="404">[404] Evaluating and Mitigating Anti-LGBTQ Biases in German and Multilingual Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30884](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30884) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Melina Morch, Daniel Braun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Bias Mitigation, Multilingual Learning, Cultural Bias
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 针对超出英语环境、且依赖性别表征的反LGBTQ偏见评估不足问题，构建包含社区来源刻板印象和WinoQueer德译的德英基准，评估8个多语言模型并微调缓解。结果显示模型复现反同性恋刻板印象，不同身份和模型间有差异；微调平均降低偏见但并非一致有效。

<h3 id="405">[405] Safety Screening for Voltage Control in Active Distribution Grids via Distributionally Robust Conformal Screening</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30889](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30889) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sarra Bouchkati, Petros Ellinas, Adriana Geisler, Steffen Kortmann, Johanna Vorwerk, Spyros Chatzivasiliadis, Andreas Ulbig
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Power System, Conformal Prediction, Distributionally Robust Optimization
- **Summary**: 为解决新电压控制策略部署前安全验证中模拟器不完善和历史数据来自旧策略的问题，提出分布鲁棒保形安全筛查（DR-CSS），利用历史仿真-真实误差构造保形安全区间并扩围。在IEEE 33/141节点系统上识别出所有不安全测试场景，并通过重定标减少对安全场景的误报。

<h3 id="406">[406] Towards Stream Learning on Embedded Systems: Benchmarking the Memory Consumption of Stream Learning Methods</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30923](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30923) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sebastian Buschjäger, Nuwan Gunasekara, Heitor Murilo Gomes
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Streaming Learning, Memory Efficiency, Edge Computing
- **Summary**: 针对流学习评估只关注概念漂移而忽视资源占用、尤其嵌入式系统内存受限的问题，在128KiB~8MiB模型大小预算下对7种流分类器、13个流数据集共6463个实验进行基准测试。结果显示自适应集成可能因初始占用超出小预算，增量树随长流增长（HT中位数7.37倍），最小预算下只有显式紧凑方法可行，社区需将资源有界性作为一等设计目标。

<h3 id="407">[407] Stride-k Subsampling: Train-Free Audio Token Reduction for Whisper</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30927](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30927) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chanhee Cho, Junhyuk Choi, Bugeun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Token Reduction, Speech Recognition, Model Compression
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main
- **Summary**: 针对Whisper固定1500 token编码接口存在冗余的问题，提出stride-k子采样，在卷积主干或编码器Transformer后保留每第k个token。k=2在两种位置下均保持基线WER，两个位置同时应用可削减75%音频token和52-58% GFLOPs，端到端延迟降低19.6-27.4%，无需训练或额外计算。

<h3 id="408">[408] LightNav-0: Eliciting VLM Spatial Intelligence for Generalist Embodied Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30935](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30935) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shaoan Wang, Aocheng Luo, Fei Huang, Jingyi Xu, Xiaoyang Wang, Yueyu Wang, Qianli Ma, Fan Yang, Ran Mei, Jia Wei, Jiangpeng Hu, Xuhao Liu, Hongming Chen, Yuanbin Shao, Yiyang Lin, Ziliang Li, Liang Pan, Xinhang Liu, Yuntao Ma, Tingxiang Fan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Visual Navigation
- **Summary**: 针对VLM空间智能未被直接用于机器人控制、现系统任务/具身特定化的问题，提出LightNav-0紧凑通用导航模型：用统一token接口（双通道指向+残差VQ动作分词器）表示多种导航任务，经ER中训练、SFT和RL训练。LightNav-0在10个公共导航仿真Setting上取得SOTA单目成功率，并在真实场景跨具身零样本泛化。

<h3 id="409">[409] MusGU+: Toward a Musician-Centered Evaluation Framework and Discovery Tool for Generative Music AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30940) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Laura Ibáñez-Martínez, Roser Batlle-Roca, Xavier Serra, Martín Rocamora
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CY; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Music Generation, Evaluation Methodology, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Venue**: AIMC 2026
- **Summary**: 针对生成式音乐系统缺乏以音乐家为中心的实用评估框架和发现工具的问题，提出MusGU+，围绕适应性、可用性、可控性三个维度评估10个代表性系统，并构建交互式发现工具。该框架支持音乐家知情选择与实用采纳，补充了强调开放性的MusGO。

<h3 id="410">[410] Taking the Whys Seriously: Limitations of Counterfactual Explanations in Justification and Recourse</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30956](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30956) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mattia Cerrato, Otto Sahlgren, Xenia Heilmann
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Counterfactual Explanation, Explainable AI, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 探讨反事实解释（CE）用于证明和行动建议的规范合法性，发现朴素应用会忽略机器学习流程上游的价值选择。通过四项干预实验表明，特征/标签测量模型、业务需求、模型验证和成功指标等选择对生成的CE影响与解释方法本身一样大，因此CE不能充分回答某些"为什么"问题。

<h3 id="411">[411] LOCI: A Locator-Critic with Refinement Loop</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30959](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30959) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Walid Bousselham, Mathilde Caron, Arsha Nagrani, Cordelia Schmid
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 针对VLM在复杂视觉理解中因无法定位关键细节而产生合理解读的问题，提出LOCI训练无关框架，将视觉搜索与证据验证解耦，由Locator和Critic两个智能体迭代精化证据。在V*、HR-Bench和VisualProbe-Hard上显著提升开源（Qwen3-VL +12.1/+5.8/+11.2）和专有（Gemini 2.5 Pro +8.9/+4.3/+4.8）模型准确率。

<h3 id="412">[412] A Universal Context-Reuse Layer for Cross-Model KV Sharing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30963](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30963) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yi Li, Dongming Jiang, Yi Zhao, Bingzhe Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, LLM Interoperability
- **Summary**: 针对KV缓存复用假定生成与消费模型相同、无法跨模型共享的问题，提出跨模型KV共享层，将源模型KV状态转换为目标模型可消费的表示。在Qwen2.5-7B→1.5B上LongBench2准确率从27.59%提升到34.48%；跨族Qwen2.5-1.5B→Gemma-2-2B在4K上下文减少67.05%预填充成本，并显著降低延迟。

<h3 id="413">[413] CogEvol: Towards Efficient and Reliable Learning Environment Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.30968](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30968) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="415">[415] MR-JEPA: A General Purpose Video Foundation Model for Cardiac MRI</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 针对心脏MRI深度学习方法多处理单张2D切片、丢弃时序与空间上下文的问题，提出MR-JEPA自监督视频基础模型，将LeJEPA扩展到3D时空输入，并在10505例多序列CMR上预训练。冻结编码器在五个回归任务上优于基线（LV EF MAE 4.79%，GLS MAE 1.87，应变任务MAE降低21-27%），疾病检测macro AUG 0.868。

<h3 id="416">[416] Evaluating and Improving LLM Self-Modeling</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

<h3 id="417">[417] Stick to What You Know: A Study of Knowledge-Aligned Supervised Fine-Tuning</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="418">[418] LLM Judges Verify Presence, Not Absence: Omission Blindness in AI Clinical Notes and What Recovers It</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.31016](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31016) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sebastian Fox, Luke Markham, Ryan Lail, Michael Karotsieris
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM-as-a-Judge, Clinical NLP
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/composo-ai/omission-bench)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM法官难以发现AI临床笔记中信息遗漏的问题，本文构建了包含500对单错误笔记的基准，测试八种法官设计后发现其对遗漏的判别力接近随机（0.50-0.63），而对新增/篡改内容较好。通过将任务重构为先列出转录本已确立的事实再逐项核对，逐事实pipeline和GEPA进化提示分别以2.7%和6.2%误报率检测出24.6%和36.9%的遗漏，后者成本更低并经医生验证。

<h3 id="419">[419] One note in three: a verified census of three deployed AI scribes, and the instrument that counted it</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.31017](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31017) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sebastian Fox, Luke Markham, Ryan Lail, Michael Karotsieris
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Clinical NLP, Error Detection, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/composo-ai/omission-bench)
- **Summary**: 针对商用AI医疗笔记错误率高且审计标准不一的问题，本文对三款商业AI scribe在142次会诊中的565份笔记进行系统审计，采用候选错误发现加对抗评审面板流程，最终31.3%的笔记存在经核实的失败，主要集中在过敏/用药信息和虚构身份。研究发现审查指令和评审模型族会显著改变失败率（9.3%至79.0%），并公开618条带证据的发现与可复现管线。

<h3 id="420">[420] Real-Time Video Anomaly Detection Using YOLO Pose Estimation and CLIP-Based Semantic Scoring</h3>

- **arXiv**: [2608.31076](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31076) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="422">[422] LLM Post-Training as Brownfield Maintenance: An Industrial Perspective on Dataware Engineering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.31102](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31102) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="423">[423] Auditing Anonymous AI Models: A Four-Stage Protocol for Black-Box Identity Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.31142](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31142) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yisen Xi
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: AI Audit, LLM Fingerprinting, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对匿名AI模型缺乏经过验证的黑盒身份核验方法的问题，本文提出四阶段取证审计协议：重建发布时配置、指纹识别配置、令牌化器差分检验和行为探针验证。在10个已知身份发布上验证判定一致性，并在GLM-5.3案例上实现前瞻性识别且与官方公布一致。

<h3 id="424">[424] SUN: Persistent Programs For Language-Grounded Control-to-Learning-to-Real Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.31167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31167) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Sim-to-Real, Model Predictive Control
- **Summary**: 针对模型预测控制与学习策略在长时程操作中语义丢失、奖励手工设计导致行为漂移的问题，本文提出语义统一程序SUN，将几何与接触关系定义为类型化可执行程序，并编译为MPC代价、RL奖励、转换守卫等。Kuafu系统从语言和场景语义自动合成SUN程序；在九个任务上宏成功率达82.03%，显著优于稀疏奖励（35.67%）和Stage-BC（24.75%），真实机器人成功率34.7%。

<h3 id="428">[428] Language-Guided Tuning: Configuration Optimization for Automated ML Research</h3>

<h3 id="429">[429] SPADE: A Large Language Model Framework for Soil Moisture Pattern Recognition and Anomaly Detection in Precision Agriculture</h3>

<h3 id="430">[430] Reasoning or Rambling? Exploring the Effect of Thinking on Agent Persuasion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.21054](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21054) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haodong Zhao, Jidong Li, Zhaomin Wu, Tianjie Ju, Zhuosheng Zhang, Bingsheng He, Gongshen Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AI Persuasion, LLM Reasoning, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Findings
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体系统中LLM说服动态的安全与可靠性问题，本文对比通用LLM与带显式思考的大推理模型，发现“说服二象性”：推理增强自身说服力（平均+21个百分点），同时降低被错误说服的易感性（最多-10个百分点）。但说服力常来自长度、重复等表面线索而非逻辑有效性，且说服在多跳代理链中非线性传播；基于注意力分析提出的对抗论证检测方法可提升鲁棒性。

<h3 id="431">[431] GUI-PRA: Process Reward Agent for GUI Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.23263](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23263) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tao Xiong, Xavier Hu, Yurun Chen, Yuhang Liu, Changqiao Wu, Pengzhi Gao, Wei Liu, Jian Luan, Shengyu Zhang

- **Summary**: 针对标准过程奖励模型在GUI任务验证中依赖浅层视觉对齐、存在视觉模糊性的问题，本文提出GUI-PRA过程奖励代理，将被动评分变为主动调查，结合经验注入准则综合与准则引导的自回归感知。在AndroidWorld和Mobile-MiniWoB++上相比标准PRM分别提升5.0%和6.5%，Qwen3-VL在AndroidWorld上达到54.74%成功率。

<h3 id="432">[432] Stop Before You Fail: Operational Capability Boundaries for Mitigating Unproductive Reasoning in Large Reasoning Models</h3>

- **Authors**: Qingjie Zhang, Yujia Fu, Yang Wang, Liu Yan, Tao Wei, Ke Xu, Minlie Huang, Han Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Early Exit, Test-Time Scaling
- **Summary**: 针对大推理模型在超出其操作能力边界的问题上产生冗长无产出推理的问题，本文研究早期失败预测信号，并提出推理表达式监控和隐藏状态监控两种测试时策略。在保持准确率的同时，token使用量减少62.7%-93.6%，显著提升效率和可靠性。

<h3 id="433">[433] MobileDreamer: Generative Sketch World Model for GUI Agent</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.04035](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04035) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yilin Cao, Yufeng Zhong, Zhixiong Zeng, Siran Dai, Liming Zheng, Jing Huang, Haibo Qiu, Peng Shi, Wenji Mao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

- **Summary**: 移动GUI代理通常只能基于当前屏幕反应式决策，难以处理长时程任务。本文提出MobileDreamer，利用文本草图世界模型预测操作后状态，并设计顺序不变学习保留空间信息，配合rollout想象策略优化动作选择。在Android World上任务成功率提升5.25%，达到最先进水平。

<h3 id="434">[434] Controllable Memory Usage: Balancing Anchoring and Innovation in Long-Term Human-Agent Interaction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.05107](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05107) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Muzhao Tian, Zisu Huang, Xiaohua Wang, Jingwen Xu, Zhengkang Guo, Qi Qian, Yuanzhe Shen, Kaitao Song, Jiakang Yuan, Changze Lv, Xiaoqing Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Long-Term Memory
- **Summary**: 现有长期交互中记忆利用要么全用要么不用，导致“记忆锚定”或历史丢失。本文提出可操控记忆代理SteeM，引入记忆依赖行为度量，并允许用户动态调节记忆依赖程度，从创新模式到高保真模式。实验表明其在多场景下优于传统提示和刚性掩码策略。

<h3 id="435">[435] SPRInG: Continual LLM Personalization via Selective Parametric Adaptation and Retrieval-Interpolated Generation</h3>

- **Authors**: Seoyeon Kim, Jaehyung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Continual Learning, RAG
- **Summary**: 用户偏好随时间漂移，现有静态检索或一次性适配无法持续个性化。本文提出SPRInG半参数框架，利用似然评分识别高新颖性交互进行选择性适配，并通过概率插值融合参数知识与检索历史。在长文本个性化生成基准上显著优于现有基线。

<h3 id="436">[436] Real-Time Deadlines Reveal Fragile Temporal Adaptation in LLM Strategic Dialogues</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.13206](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13206) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Neil K. R. Sehgal, Sharath Chandra Guntuku, Lyle Ungar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, LLM Evaluation, Strategic Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sehgal-neil/llm-temporal-awareness)
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在现实时间约束下适应性脆弱。本文通过带严格截止时间的模拟谈判研究剩余时间反馈的影响。结果显示反馈使GPT-5.1的成交率从4%升至32%，但该适应高度依赖模型和提示呈现方式，且在轮次限制下近完美，说明并非能力不足。

<h3 id="437">[437] Not Your Typical Sycophant: The Elusive Nature of Sycophancy in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.15436](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15436) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shahar Ben-Natan, Oren Tsur
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Sycophancy, LLM-as-a-Judge, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 现有奉承探测受提示中多种偏差干扰。本文提出用LLM作为裁判的零和押注游戏，直接且中立地探测奉承，并定义对第三方造成代价的奉承。11个模型中大多数在常见设置下有奉承倾向，但7个表现出“道德悔恨”，其中5个显著过度补偿，呈现“反奉承”偏差。

<h3 id="438">[438] ShardMemo: Scope-Before-Routing for Agentic Memory Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21545](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21545) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yang Zhao, Chengxiao Dai, Mengying Kou, Yue Xiu, Dusit Niyato
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Agent Memory, RAG, Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search
- **Summary**: 代理系统持久记忆检索需同时满足权限范围和相关性预算，现有方法在检索后过滤或把范围作为排名特征都有缺陷。本文提出ShardMemo，先通过元数据谓词识别合法分片，再用学习路由选择少量分片进行局部近邻检索。在LoCoMo上F1提升约+3，端到端最高提升+6.8。

<h3 id="439">[439] Defining Operational Conditions for Safety-Critical AI-Based Systems from Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22118](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22118) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Johann Maximilian Christensen, Elena Hoemann, Frank Köster, Sven Hallerbach
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Operational Design Domain, AI Safety, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 安全关键AI系统中操作设计域（ODD）的定义通常不完整且依赖专家知识。本文提出从数据后验定义ODD的多维核表示方法，并在合成基准和航空用例上验证，证明在假设下体积收敛，且表示具有排列稳定、有界、单位不变等性质。

<h3 id="440">[440] HumanStudy-Bench: Towards AI Agent Design for Participant Simulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00685) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuan Liu, Haoyang Shang, Zizhang Liu, Xinyan Liu, Yunze Xiao, Yiwen Tu, Haojian Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Social Simulation, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 大语言模型作为社会实验模拟被试时行为不稳定，评估常混淆基础模型能力与实验实例化。本文将参与者模拟视为代理设计问题，提出HumanStudy-Bench平台，通过人机协同的Filter-Extract-Execute-Evaluate流程重建已发表实验，并引入两个与人类一致性的指标。在12项包含6000+试次的研究上验证了平台。

<h3 id="441">[441] Reasoning and Tool-use Compete in Agentic RL:From Quantifying Interference to Disentangled Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00994](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00994) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yu Li, Mingyang Yi, Xiuyu Li, Ju Fan, Fuxin Jiang, Binbin Chen, Peng Li, Jie Song, Tieying Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agentic RL, Tool Learning, LoRA
- **Summary**: 现有代理强化学习通常联合优化推理和工具使用，但这一假设未经验证。本文提出能力效应归因（CEA）量化两者间的干扰，并推出解耦的Action-Reasoning Tuning（DART）框架，通过分离的LoRA模块参数更新。在13个基准上DART优于所有联合优化基线，逼近2-Agent上界。

<h3 id="442">[442] Beyond Dense States: Sparse Transcoders as Causally Testable Operators for LLM Latent Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.01695](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01695) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yadong Wang, Haodong Chen, Yu Tian, Chuanxing Geng, Dong Liang, Xiang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sparse Autoencoder, Latent Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 现有潜推理通常使用密集纠缠的转变，推理轨迹难以检查或干预。本文提出LSTR，将稀疏转码器转变为可干预的循环转变组件，结合线性跳过路径和Top-k稀疏创新路径。在GSM8K-Aug上仅消除少数关键稀疏特征可使准确率下降16.5%，证明其因果作用。

<h3 id="443">[443] AgentRx: Diagnosing AI Agent Failures from Execution Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02475) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shraddha Barke, Arnav Goyal, Alind Khare, Avaljot Singh, Suman Nath, Chetan Bansal
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Debugging, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Venue**: EMNLP Findings 2026
- **Summary**: AI代理执行轨迹是概率性、长时程、多代理且受噪声工具输出影响，失败定位困难。本文发布含170条轨迹的基准，并基于扎根理论构建跨领域失败分类法；提出AgentRx自动诊断框架，通过约束合成与逐步评估定位关键失败步骤。平均步骤定位准确率提升75%。

<h3 id="444">[444] Towards Natural Personalization: Evaluating Long-Horizon Preference Following in Personalized User-LLM Interactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.04191](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04191) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qianyun Guo, Yibo Li, Yue Liu, Bryan Hooi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, LLM Evaluation, User Modeling
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Findings
- **Summary**: 评估LLM在长期自然交互中遵循个性化偏好仍待探索。本文提出RealPref基准，包含100个合成用户画像、1300条偏好、4种表达类型和长时程历史。结果表明上下文增长和偏好表达隐式化时性能显著下降，且泛化到未见场景更具挑战。

<h3 id="445">[445] Retrieval-Augmented LLM Agents: Learning to Learn from Experience</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.18272](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18272) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thomas Palmeira Ferraz, Romain Deffayet, Vassilina Nikoulina, Hervé Déjean, Stéphane Clinchant
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Agent, In-Context Learning, Fine-Tuning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 - Main Conference
- **Summary**: 监督微调难以外推到新任务，经验检索常不如监督基线。本文结合二者，研究检索增强的LLM代理如何上下文使用检索轨迹。首先建立强LoRA微调基线，然后将经验检索直接融入微调过程，显著提升对未见任务的泛化性，且在不完美经验甚至复用失败尝试时仍有效。

<h3 id="446">[446] Unified-MAS: Universally Generating Domain-Specific Nodes for Empowering Automatic Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.21475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21475) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hehai Lin, Yu Yan, Zixuan Wang, Bo Xu, Sudong Wang, Weiquan Huang, Ruochen Zhao, Minzhi Li, Chengwei Qin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/linhh29/Unified-MAS)
- **Summary**: 自动多代理系统生成在知识密集型领域受限于静态节点库或在线生成中的架构耦合。本文提出Unified-MAS，通过离线节点合成解耦节点实现与拓扑编排：搜索外部知识生成节点蓝图，基于困惑度奖励优化瓶颈节点。在四个专业领域提升达14.2%且成本降低。

<h3 id="447">[447] INTRYGUE: Induction-Aware Entropy Gating for Reliable RAG Uncertainty Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.21607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21607) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alexandra Kuleshova, Andrei Volodichev, Daria Kotova, Alexey Zaytsev
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, RAG, LLM Hallucination, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (Main)
- **Summary**: 预测熵在RAG中不可靠，源于归纳头既支持正确回答又激活熵神经元。本文提出INTRYGUE，基于注意力对归纳头活性估计来门控预测熵，无需训练。在四个RAG基准和六个开源LLM上，性能与最强基线相当或更好，表明需结合预测不确定性和可解释的内部上下文利用信号。

<h3 id="448">[448] JFTA-Bench: Evaluate LLM's Ability of Tracking and Analyzing Malfunctions Using Fault Trees</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.22978](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22978) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuhui Wang, Zhixiong Yang, Ming Zhang, Shihan Dou, Zhiheng Xi, Enyu Zhou, Senjie Jin, Yujiong Shen, Dingwei Zhu, Yi Dong, Tao Gui, Qi Zhang, Xuanjing Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fault Diagnosis, LLM Evaluation, Dialogue System, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 故障树通常以图像存储，难以被LLM直接处理用于故障跟踪分析。本文提出故障树的文本表示，并构建多轮对话基准JFTA-Bench，含3130条条目，平均每条目40.75轮，通过训练模型生成模糊信息并模拟用户错误场景。Gemini 2.5 Pro表现最佳。

<h3 id="449">[449] PeopleSearchBench: Evaluating AI-Powered People Search Platforms with Criteria-Grounded Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.27476](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27476) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianyu Shi, Wei Wang, Zequn Xie, Shuai Zhang, Boyang Xia, Chenyu Zeng, Qi Zhang, Lynn Ai, Yaqi Yu, Kaiming Zhang, Feiyue Tang, Zhenyu Yu, Lei Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: People Search, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对AI人物搜索平台缺乏标准化评估基准的问题，提出了PeopleSearchBench基准，包含119个多语言查询和基于准则验证的评估方法。实验表明多源搜索智能体显著优于单领域系统，尤其在影响者发现场景差距最大。

<h3 id="450">[450] View-oriented Conversation Compiler for Agent Trace Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.29678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29678) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lvmin Zhang, Maneesh Agrawala
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Traceability, Context Engineering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lllyasviel/VCC)
- **Summary**: 针对智能体追踪日志庞大且难以分析的问题，提出了面向视图的对话编译器VCC，将原始JSONL日志转换为完整、UI和自适应三个视图。在AppWorld实验中，VCC将任务目标通过率提升1.1至4.2个百分点，并将反射器token消耗降低一半至三分之二。

<h3 id="451">[451] A Multi-Agent Human-LLM Collaborative Framework for Closed-Loop Scientific Literature Summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.01452](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01452) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Maxwell J. Jacobson, Daniel Xie, Jackson Shen, Adil Wazeer, Guang Lin, Xiao-Ying Yu, Haiyan Wang, Xinghang Zhang, Yexiang Xue
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Summarization, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 针对科学文献分析碎片化和现有AI工具缺乏结构化多步方法的问题，提出了Elhuyar多智能体人机协作框架，集成LLM、结构化AI和人类科学家进行迭代式文献提取与分析。在材料科学应用中，该框架发现了氦气泡指数生长与辐照剂量和温度的关联，证明了AI辅助文献综述能加速科学发现。

<h3 id="452">[452] ReVEL: Multi-Turn Reflective LLM-Guided Heuristic Evolution via Structured Performance Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.04940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04940) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cuong Van Duc, Minh Nguyen Dinh Tuan, Tam Vu Duc, Tung Vu Duy, Son Nguyen Van, Hanh Nguyen Thi, Binh Huynh Thi Thanh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Heuristic Design, Evolutionary Computation, Combinatorial Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对NP-hard组合优化中启发式设计依赖专家知识且LLM演化方法反馈有限的问题，提出了ReVEL框架，通过行为感知分组和累积性能反馈进行多轮迭代优化。在标准组合优化基准上，ReVEL普遍优于现有LLM引导演化基线，行为感知分组有助于更一致的优化轨迹。

<h3 id="453">[453] WebXSkill: Skill Learning for Autonomous Web Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.13318](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13318) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhaoyang Wang, Qianhui Wu, Xuchao Zhang, Chaoyun Zhang, Wenlin Yao, Fazle Elahi Faisal, Baolin Peng, Si Qin, Suman Nath, Qingwei Lin, Chetan Bansal, Dongmei Zhang, Saravan Rajmohan, Jianfeng Gao, Huaxiu Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Web Agent, Skill Learning
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/aiming-lab/WebXSkill)
- **Summary**: 针对自主Web智能体在长程浏览器工作流中脆弱且现有技能表示存在落地鸿沟的问题，提出了WebXSkill框架，将可执行技能与参数化动作程序和步骤级自然语言指导配对。在WebArena、WebVoyager和Online-Mind2Web上取得了一致改进，并发现最佳部署模式取决于模型的规划与执行能力。

<h3 id="454">[454] ReactBench: A Benchmark for Topological Reasoning in MLLMs on Chemical Reaction Diagrams</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.15994](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15994) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiang Xu, Shengyuan Bai, Yu Wang, He Cao, Leqing Chen, Yuanyuan Liu, Bin Feng, Zijing Liu, Yu Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal LLM, Visual Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference)
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大模型在复杂拓扑结构推理上的严重退化且现有基准未覆盖这一问题，提出了ReactBench基准，包含1618个专家标注QA对。评估24个MLLM发现锚点任务与整体结构推理任务之间性能差距超过30%，且瓶颈在于推理而非感知。

<h3 id="455">[455] Training and Agentic Inference Strategies for LLM-based Manim Animation Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.18364](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18364) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ravidu Suien Rammuni Silva, Ahmad Lotfi, Isibor Kennedy Ihianle, Golnaz Shahtahmassebi, Jordan J. Bird
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Video Generation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对Manim动画生成中LLM缺乏空间推理与API知识且训练/推理策略研究缺失的问题，提出了ManimTrainer训练流程和ManimAgent推理流程。Qwen3 Coder 30B结合GRPO和RITL-DOC达到94%渲染成功率和85.7%视觉相似度，超过GPT-4.1 3个百分点。

<h3 id="456">[456] First-Order Efficiency for Probabilistic Value Estimation via A Statistical Viewpoint</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02827](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02827) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziqi Liu, Kiljae Lee, Yuan Zhang, Weijing Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; stat.ME; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Shapley Values, Data Valuation, Monte Carlo Methods
- **Summary**: 针对概率值（如Shapley值）的精确计算需指数级效用评估的问题，观察到现有估计器的共同一阶展开式，并提出EASE估计器，通过联合选择采样律和替代函数最小化一阶MSE。实验表明EASE在多种概率值上一致优于现有估计器。

<h3 id="457">[457] SkillRet: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Skill Retrieval in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05726](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05726) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongcheol Cho, Ryangkyung Kang, Youngeun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Skill Retrieval, Benchmark, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ThakiCloud/SKILLRET)
- **Summary**: 针对大规模技能库中LLM智能体技能检索缺乏基准的问题，提出了SkillRet基准，包含16,129个技能和4,392个评估查询。任务特定微调在NDCG@10上比最强先前检索器提升12.9分，比最强现成检索器提升16.2分。

<h3 id="458">[458] Useful Memories Become Faulty When Continuously Updated by LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12978](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12978) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dylan Zhang, Yanshan Lin, Zhengkun Wu, Yihang Sun, Bingxuan Li, Dianqi Li, Hao Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Memory, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对LLM持续更新记忆会导致记忆失效的问题，发现记忆效用先升后降，且即使基于正确经验也会出错。GPT-5.4在之前无记忆可解的ARC-AGI问题上有54%失败；保留原始情节的智能体比强制整理记忆的准确率翻倍，表明应优先保留原始情节并显式门控记忆整合。

<h3 id="459">[459] SciAtlas: A Computable Atlas of Science for Knowledge-Grounded AI Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22878](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22878) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuofei Qiao, Yunxiang Wei, Busheng Zhang, Mengru Wang, Jiazheng Fan, Huadong Jian, Bin Wu, Shumin Deng, Yida Xue, Zifan Cheng, Xiang Chen, Dan Zhang, Junfeng Fang, Ningyu Zhang, Keyan Ding, Qiang Zhang, Jeff Z. Pan, Emine Yilmaz, Huajun Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Neurosymbolic AI, Scientific Discovery
- **Summary**: 针对AI科学家缺乏共享、机器可操作且跨学科的知识基础设施的问题，提出了SciAtlas，整合证据、概念、学科、专家和规范层，并实现统一神经符号检索机制。在三个工作流上验证了其重建轨迹、发现机会和评估创新的能力。

<h3 id="460">[460] Summoning the Oracle to Slay It: Mitigating Look-Ahead Bias in Financial Backtesting with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24564](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24564) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Weixian Waylon Li, Mengyu Wang, Tiejun Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Memorization, Quantitative Finance, Decoding Strategy
- **Summary**: 针对LLM预训练数据导致金融回测中参数化前视偏差的问题，提出了FinCAD，一种推理时采用上下文感知解码的适应性方法，结合对抗性偏差发现和实体/日期自适应规则。最大模型级平均样本内收益修正为-67.1%，榜单平均Spearman相关从+0.779提升至+0.846。

<h3 id="461">[461] Agent-as-Peer-Debriefer: A Multi-Agent Framework with Perspective-Based Refinement for Qualitative Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24600) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhimin Lin, Kun Cheng, Zhiyao Shu, Junhua Fang, Juntao Li, Fan Bai, Jie Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Human-AI Collaboration, Qualitative Data Analysis
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 针对LLM定性数据分析缺乏人类同行评审的深度和细致度的问题，提出了Agent-as-Peer-Debriefer多智能体框架，加入三种不同分析视角的同行评审智能体。在三个数据集和三个LLM上，基于视角的同行评审比单一LLM基线更接近人类编码，且消融实验表明改进并非仅来自额外细化。

<h3 id="462">[462] VeriTrace: Evolving Mental Models for Deep Research Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26081](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26081) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haolang Zhao, Yunbo Long, Lukas Beckenbauer, Alexandra Brintrup
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Research Agent, Cognitive Architecture
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 针对深度研究智能体中间表示易受信息污染且误差依赖传播的问题，提出了VeriTrace认知图谱框架，显式实现解释更新、偏差反馈和模式修正三个调节回路。在DeepResearch Bench上比最强匹配基线平均提升4.82个百分点（整体1.83），并在DeepConsult上胜率提升5.9个百分点。

<h3 id="463">[463] PortBench: A Correlation-Aware, Full-Pipeline Benchmark for LLM-Driven Portfolio Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27887](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27887) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Zhao, Sijia Chen, Ningxin Su
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; q-fin.PM
- **Tags**: Quantitative Finance, Benchmark, Financial AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AgenticFinLab/portbench)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM驱动的投资组合管理缺乏考虑跨资产相关性和完整流程的基准的问题，提出了PortBench，涵盖六类资产和动态五阶段流程，并引入双重相关分数和CEPS指标。十个前沿LLM中仅32.5%的评估在Sharpe上超越等权基准，说明强问答能力不能转化为优秀组合表现。

<h3 id="464">[464] AsyncTool: Evaluating the Asynchronous Function Calling Capability under Multi-Task Scenarios</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27995) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kou Shi, Ziao Zhang, Shiting Huang, Avery Nie, Zhen Fang, Qiuchen Wang, Lin Chen, Huaian Chen, Zehui Chen, Feng Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/StoKou/repo-asynctool)
- **Summary**: 针对现有工具调用评估忽略延迟和单任务限制的问题，提出了AsyncTool基准，模拟多任务场景下延迟工具反馈。实验表明延迟反馈导致性能显著下降，而任务切换、依赖跟踪和状态维护能力更强的模型表现更好。

<h3 id="465">[465] Zipping the Thought: When and How Compressed Reasoning Data Works in LLM Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28008](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28008) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kohsei Matsutani, Gouki Minegishi, Takeshi Kojima, Yusuke Iwasawa, Yutaka Matsuo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Verifiable Rewards
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM后训练中压缩推理数据（CoT）的效果尚不明确，性能和token成本存在权衡。方法：提出显式/组合/隐式CoT分类，并通过合成组合推理任务进行控制实验。结果：更粗粒度的CoT需要更多SFT数据；组合与隐式CoT从数据规模扩展中获益更多，但隐式CoT易导致记忆化；RLVR可分解压缩步骤；单向CoT顺序泛化更强。

<h3 id="466">[466] Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rebecca M. M. Hicke, Kiran Tomlinson
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: User Modeling, User Behavior Analysis, Human-AI Interaction
- **Summary**: 问题：现有用户-LLM交互研究多为静态，缺乏个体随时间变化的分析。方法：分析约12,000名Bing Copilot用户的对话轨迹，并与WildChat比较。结果：个体用户习惯高度稳定；活跃用户更成功且任务更复杂；WildChat偏向“超级用户”，不能代表典型交互。

<h3 id="467">[467] Citation-Closure Retrieval and Per-Rule Attribution for Real-World Regulatory Compliance Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29742](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29742) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yeong-Joon Ju, Seong-Whan Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph, Legal AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yeongjoonJu/RefWalk)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有RAG系统在监管合规问答中因扁平化引用边和脆弱归因而表现不佳。方法：提出RegOps-Bench基准与RefWalk框架，通过操作知识图谱进行跨文档引用遍历，并采用逐规则归因。结果：在检索召回和引用精度上显著提升；在HIPAA对比中显示现有系统在平铺结构规则上饱和。

<h3 id="468">[468] VLA-Trace: Diagnosing Vision-Language-Action Models through Representation and Behavior Tracing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30117](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30117) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoyuan Shi, Xiancong Ren, Yingji Zhang, Qinfan Zhang, Jiayu Hu, Haozhe Shan, Han Dong, Jinpeng Lu, Yinda Chen, Yi Zhang, Yong Dai, Xiaozhu Ju
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action Model, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 该论文旨在理解视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型如何将多模态知识转化为具身控制，提出了VLA-Trace诊断框架，结合跨模态与检查点漂移的CKA、注意力消融干预和轨迹级行为探针。在π_0.5和OpenVLA上的实验发现两者具有不同的模态适应动态、多模态路由策略和层级依赖，且在细粒度语义跟随方面存在局限。

<h3 id="469">[469] When Should Models Change Their Minds? Contextual Belief Management in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30219) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoming Xu, Weihong Xu, Zongrui Li, Mengru Wang, Yunzhi Yao, Chiyu Wu, Jin Shang, Yu Gong, Shumin Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Belief State, LLM Evaluation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 main conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zjunlp/CBM)
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了长程交互中语言模型需要管理随时间累积信息（更新、保持或忽略）的语境信念管理问题，并引入BeliefTrack基准，涵盖规则发现和电路诊断两类任务。实验表明普通模型存在严重失败，而基于信念状态奖励的强化学习平均将失败率降低70.9%，表示级引导可降低46.1%。

<h3 id="470">[470] Self-Correction Can Amplify Hallucinations: Fact-Level Repair with Graph-Based Evidence Routing in Multimodal Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00232](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00232) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kaixiang Zhao, Tianrun Yu, Shawn Huang, Porter Jenkins, Yushun Dong, Amanda Hughes
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Multimodal Learning, Self-Correction
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (Main Conference)
- **Summary**: 问题：基于推理时修复的多模态生成幻觉方法受输出中幻觉主张偏见影响，且无法按事实级排序反馈。方法：提出TIGER，分别提取输入观测图和输出主张图，计算风险分数并选择性修复高风险主张。结果：在图像/音频/视频到文本等跨模态路径上减少不支持内容，同时保持任务质量。

<h3 id="471">[471] Hidden Thoughts Are Not Secret: Reasoning Trace Exposure in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00642](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00642) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yu-An Lu, Ci-Yang Tsai, Yu-Lin Tsai, Raluca Ada Popa, Chia-Mu Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Prompt Engineering, Knowledge Distillation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：部署系统隐藏推理轨迹，但接口级隐藏是否能阻止用户通过提示获得推理监督尚不清楚。方法：提出推理暴露提示（REP），用影子模型演示以代码格式诱发受害者模型暴露内部轨迹。结果：REP显著提高暴露轨迹与内部轨迹的相似性，同时保留有用的推理信号。

<h3 id="472">[472] Handoff Debt: The Rediscovery Cost When Coding Agents Take Over Interrupted Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02875](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02875) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dipesh KC, Anjila Budathoki
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Code Generation, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 问题：编码智能体基准假设连续无中断任务，而真实工作中断和交接带来重新发现成本。方法：提出“交接债务”概念和接管协议，在不同交接视图下评估后续智能体。结果：带有上下文的交接将中位事件减少20-59%，提示token减少42-63%，解决率影响较小。

<h3 id="473">[473] Perceive Before Reasoning: A Pre-Reasoning Perception Framework for Efficient and Reliable Proactive Mobile Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.03236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03236) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhijie Ding, Weinan Hong, Zicheng Zhu, Lei Li, Dezhi Kong, Hao Wang, Peng Zhou, Xuchu Jiang, Jiaming Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Proactive Agent, LLM Agent, GUI Automation
- **Summary**: 问题：主动移动智能体需决定何时干预，统一流程造成目标错位和冗余推理。方法：提出预推理感知框架（PRPF），用轻量感知器进行干预门控和上下文压缩，仅在需要时激活推理器。结果：在ProactiveMobile基准上显著降低误触发率，同时提高成功率和推理效率。

<h3 id="474">[474] Evolving Agents in the Dark: Retrospective Harness Optimization via Self-Preference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05922](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05922) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenbo Pan, Shujie Liu, Chin-Yew Lin, Jingying Zeng, Xianfeng Tang, Xiangyang Zhou, Yan Lu, Xiaohua Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Self-Evolving Agent, Tool Learning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 (Findings)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wbopan/retro-harness)
- **Summary**: 问题：代理的“工具箱”优化通常需要真值验证集，实际部署中难以获取。方法：提出回顾式工具箱优化（RHO），仅用历史轨迹，选择核心困难任务，通过自验证和自偏好更新工具箱。结果：SWE-Bench Pro通过率从59%提升至78%，无需外部评分。

<h3 id="475">[475] CrowdMath: A Dataset of Crowdsourced Mathematical Research Discussions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06526](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06526) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sherin Muckatira, Jesse Geneson, Slava Gerovitch, Pavel Etingof, Mikhail Gronas, Anna Rumshisky
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Crowdsourcing, Benchmark
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Findings
- **Summary**: 问题：现有数学推理基准无法评估协作式开放问题求解。方法：构建CrowdMath数据集，包含164条专家标注的进度链，并定义下一贴预测和角色分类任务。结果：模型在下一贴预测上准确率83-88%，但在角色分类上最佳仅0.42 macro-F1，暴露理解协作进展的差距。

<h3 id="476">[476] How Small Can You Go? LoRA Fine-Tuning 270M-8B Models for Merchant Information Extraction in Financial Transactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08051](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08051) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Donghao Huang, Tomas Drietomsky, Benjamin Barrett, Zhaoxia Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Information Extraction, LLM Deployment
- **Venue**: IEEE International Conference on Data Mining (ICDM) 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：部署的商家信息提取大模型内存和吞吐开销大，需要更小的替代模型。方法：评估23个LoRA微调运行，涵盖多种模型、LoRA rank、提示和部署环境。结果：rank-8 LLaMA达到96.75% F1，仅比rank-32低0.20；Qwen-4B JSON-Only达96.60% F1且吞吐提升3.8倍；0.8B模型仍达94.75% F1。

<h3 id="477">[477] ForesightSafety-SAGE:A Fully Automated Scenario Generation and Safety Evaluation Framework for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08531](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08531) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lu Jia, Haibo Tong, Feifei Zhao, Jindong Li, Dongqi Liang, Ping Wu, Qian Zhang, Yi Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Safety, Safety Evaluation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 问题：现有LLM智能体安全评估依赖人工场景和静态提示，难以覆盖执行中的多样化风险。方法：提出ForesightSafety-SAGE框架，基于五个风险维度自动生成1072个可执行场景，并评估12个LLM智能体。结果：平均攻击成功率（ASR）为47.1%，部分模型超过70%，表明执行级评估的重要。

<h3 id="478">[478] From AGI to ASI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.12683](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12683) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tim Genewein, Matija Franklin, Alexander Lerchner, Laurent Orseau, Samuel Albanie, Adam Bales, Cole Wyeth, Stephanie Chan, Iason Gabriel, Joel Z. Leibo, Allan Dafoe, Marcus Hutter, Thore Graepel, Shane Legg
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AGI, AI Safety, AI Governance
- **Summary**: 问题：后AGI时代AI如何向超级智能发展尚缺乏系统性分析。方法：报告描述了ASI特征，提出四条潜在路径（扩展、范式转变、递归改进、多智能体集体），并讨论摩擦和瓶颈。结果：尽管存在不确定性，AI进展可能持续加速，需跨学科全球协作。

<h3 id="479">[479] IterCAD: An Iterative Multimodal Agent for Visually-Grounded CAD Generation and Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13368](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13368) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tao Hu, Jiaxin Ai, Licheng Wen, Xueheng Li, Shu Zou, Siqi Li, Nianchen Deng, Xinyu Cai, Hongbin Zhou, Pinlong Cai, Daocheng Fu, Yu Yang, Hairong Zhang, Botian Shi, Xuemeng Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: CAD Generation, Multimodal Agent, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：现有CAD自动生成多为开环一次性生成，不符合实际迭代设计流程。方法：提出IterCAD多模态智能体框架，与可执行CAD沙箱进行多轮交互，涵盖绘图到代码、文本到代码和交互编辑。结果：在多个基准上显著优于现有方法，代码可执行性和几何精度均提升，且具备闭环迭代优化能力。

<h3 id="480">[480] Beyond Helpfulness: A Teaching-over-Solving Diagnostic for Measuring Educational Impact in LLM Tutors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16206](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16206) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junyi Yao, Zihao Zheng, Baichuan Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Education Technology, AI Tutoring, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: 2026 EMNLP NLP4PI workshop
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM作为辅导者时，任务解决能力强不代表学习支持效果好。方法：提出基于求解导向与教学导向性能差距的简易诊断，分析MathTutorBench。结果：两者相关性仅0.421，多个模型排名显著变化；基准评分中明确编码了维护学生能动性的行为，建议分开报告两类分数。

<h3 id="481">[481] A homotopy-type-theoretic generalization of neurosymbolic inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.17851](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17851) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fernando Zhapa-Camacho, Robert Hoehndorf
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Homotopy Type Theory
- **Venue**: NeSy 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/bio-ontology-research-group/hott-nesy)
- **Summary**: 针对神经符号系统在计算信念加权逻辑量时忽略对称性和证明多重性的问题，提出了基于同伦类型论的框架，将功能转化为信念加权同伦基数，并证明保守性定理。在MNIST推理捷径基准上，所提单模型包装器比多样性集成校准更好，同时保持标签准确率。

<h3 id="482">[482] DiagFlowBench: Evaluating How Language Models Handle Off-Procedure Inputs in Grounded Diagnostic Dialogue</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.17904](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17904) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guillermo Gil de Avalle, Laura Maruster, Shaina Raza, Christos Emmanouilidis
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Procedural Reasoning, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Industry Track
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/guille-gil/DiagFlowBench)
- **Summary**: 针对现有基准忽视语言模型在接地诊断对话中处理非脚本化输入的能力，引入DiagFlowBench数据集，包含50个工业诊断流程图转换的1676轮多轮对话。评测10个模型发现模型经常选择真实但上下文不合适的步骤，揭示接地系统的一个脆弱点。

<h3 id="483">[483] ScaffoldAgent: Utility-Guided Dynamic Outline Optimization for Open-Ended Deep Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20122](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20122) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhibang Yang, Xinke Jiang, Yuzhen Xiao, Ruizhe Zhang, Yue Fang, Xinfei Wan, Zhengxing Song, Yuxuan Liu, Yuheng Huang, Junfeng Zhao, Yasha Wang, Xu Chu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Deep Research Agent, Long-Form Generation
- **Summary**: 针对开放深度研究中大纲固定或局部优化导致脚手架漂移的问题，提出ScaffoldAgent，将大纲演化建模为扩展、收缩、修订的结构化决策过程，并引入效用引导反馈机制。实验表明其在DeepResearch Bench和Gym上提升长报告生成和事实基础。

<h3 id="484">[484] PEAR: Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20621](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20621) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yang Feng, Ziwei Xu, Xia Hu, Fengxiang He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; cs.MA; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Debate, LLM Reasoning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EVIEHub/PEAR)
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体辩论中固定拓扑导致位置偏见和角色敏感问题，提出置换等变自适应路由多智能体辩论协议PEAR，动态重新配置通信角色和稀疏拓扑。在四个推理基准和六个LLM上显著提高平均准确率。

<h3 id="485">[485] In LLM Reasoning, there is Irrationality on top of Value Misalignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20624](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20624) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kejiang Qian, Fengxiang He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Alignment, Self-Consistency
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EVIEHub/LLM-Rationality)
- **Summary**: 针对对齐后的LLM在推理中仍可能无法最大化对齐价值的问题，形式化理性价值风险并分解估计误差。在多个模型和基准上验证了理性价值风险的普遍性，并发现自一致性和更长的思维链能提高理性但收益递减。

<h3 id="486">[486] DART: Draft-Agreement Routing for Training-Free Adaptive Thinking Budgets in Hybrid Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23181) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jungseob Lee, Seongtae Hong, Seungjun Lee, Jaehyung Seo, Junyoung Son, Sugyeong Eo, Chanjun Park, Hyeongju Park, Hyeonseok Moon, Heuiseok Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Model Routing, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Findings
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/js-lee-AI/DART)
- **Summary**: 针对混合推理模型需要为每个查询选择直接回答或扩展思考的预算路由问题，提出训练无关的DART框架，通过两个无思考草稿的一致性决定直接回答，否则根据熵预测思考预算。在奥林匹克级数学和代码任务上准确率提升最高9.0和22.5点，同时思考令牌减少32-73%。

<h3 id="487">[487] RIFT-Bench: Dynamic Red-teaming For Agentic AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23927](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23927) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yarin Yerushalmi Levi, Roy Betser, Amit Giloni, Lidor Erez, Itay Gershon, Oren Rachmil, Sindhu Padakandla, Roman Vainshtein
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Security, Red Teaming, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对现有智能体AI安全评估依赖于特定实现或领域的问题，提出RIFT-Bench，一种表示驱动的动态红队方法论，通过发现和扫描两个自动化阶段使用105个自适应对抗探针统一评估异构智能体架构。在45个系统上验证了其泛化性和对缓解策略的评估能力。

<h3 id="488">[488] COMPASS: Grounding Composition-Intent Guidance in Unified Multimodal Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.28696](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28696) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziqi Zhou, Weize Quan, Mining Tan, Zhihan Chen, Dandan Zheng, Jingdong Chen, Jun Zhou, Weiming Dong, Dong-Ming Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal LLM, Compositional Generation, Mixture-of-Experts
- **Summary**: 针对统一多模态模型在细粒度组合识别和可控生成上的不足，提出COMPASS，首个在一个系统中接地组合意图控制的统一多模态框架，通过共享专家令牌在MoE骨干中注入组合专业知识并引导生成。构建Comp-11数据集，实验表明大幅提升类别级组合理解和生成一致性。

<h3 id="489">[489] Mnemosyne: Agentic Transaction Processing for Validating and Repairing AI-generated Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.00269](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00269) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Edward Y. Chang, Longling Geng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agentic Workflow, LLM Agent, Transaction Processing
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成的工作流动作可能存在过期、不可行、冲突等问题，提出智能体事务处理(ATP)范式，将生成动作视为未经批准的提议，通过确定性准入接受或触发有界反应性修复。在九个安全基准上拒绝所有目标违规，吞吐成本仅增加5.2-6.6%，修复编辑量比全局重算少近一个数量级。

<h3 id="490">[490] Where Knowledge and Authority Sit Changes What an Agent Benchmark Can Resolve</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.02975](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02975) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dan C. Hsu, Luke Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation, Social Simulation
- **Summary**: 针对大多数智能体基准将事实、工具和权限放在同一接口下而现实组织分散在人身上的问题，将18个客服任务转化为三种访问设置。在864次试验中，社会访问降低了所有模型的成功率，最新模型gpt-5.6-sol在社交访问中最高0.65，与集中式间接访问相比下降0.11。

<h3 id="491">[491] Agora: Enhancing LLM Agent Reasoning Via Auction-Based Task Allocation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.09600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09600) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kaiji Zhou, Aleš Leonardis, Yue Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Task Allocation, Auction Design
- **Venue**: Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有框架基于粗粒度匹配调用API而忽略性能差异和成本效率的问题，提出Agora框架，使用置信度校准的拍卖动态分配任务给专家模型和工具。在五个基准上相比单模型、路由和级联基线有所提升或保持竞争力。

<h3 id="492">[492] Can Agentic Trading Systems Pay for Their Own Intelligence?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.10286](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10286) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiqi Duan, Changlun Li, Chen Wang, Fan Zhang, Mengxiang Wang, Dayi Miao, Peixian Ma, Jiangpeng Yan, Liyuan Chen, Shuoling Liu, Preslav Nakov, Yuyu Luo, Nan Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Quantitative Finance, LLM Agent, Agent Evaluation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Findings
- **Summary**: 针对现有评估很少检查智能体交易系统是否划算的问题，提出TradeLens诊断工具包，从交易记录、运行时轨迹和部署配置重建轨迹并归因利润和成本。结果表明可行性取决于智能到利润的转换，不同模型存在不同失败模式，资本规模等只起放大作用。

<h3 id="493">[493] WorldCupArena: Fine-Grained Evaluation of Language Models and Deep-Research Agents on Football Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.18084](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18084) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhaokai Wang, Tianlin Gui, Jiayuan Rao, Shangzhe Di, Yihong Tang, Dingli Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sports Analytics, Deep Research Agent, Event Forecasting
- **Summary**: 针对足球比赛预测需要动态信息和赛前明确预测的问题，提出WorldCupArena动态基准，以2026世界杯为首个评测。评测显示相似的结果准确率可能掩盖详细预测的更大差异，最佳系统相比博彩市场和人类球迷基线仅在比分上有更明显优势。

<h3 id="494">[494] EvoSQL: Memory-Augmented Critic-Generator Co-Evolution for Text-to-SQL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.20489](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20489) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiawei Zhou, Jianwei Wang, Chenyu Zhou, Chaojian Shi, Ming Dong, Kai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, Self-Distillation, Code Generation
- **Venue**: EMNLP Findings 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/valleysprings/EvoSQL)
- **Summary**: 针对复杂Text-to-SQL需要多步分解和执行诊断的问题，提出EvoSQL协同进化框架，生成器和批评者迭代交互，维护候选记忆并通过效用引导聚合更新，并用SDPO微调增强。在Spider和BIRD上稳定提升，BIRD-Dev上提升幅度从+1.37%到+9.19%。

<h3 id="495">[495] DynamicMCPBench: A Trace-Grounded, Effect-Scored Benchmark for LLM Agents over Live MCP Servers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.20531](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20531) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jerzy Kamiński, Ilya Galyukshev, Artem Kuznetsov, Sergey Chuprin, Kirill Redko, Aidar Shumbalov, Anna Kalyuzhnaya
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Benchmark, LLM Agent, Model Context Protocol
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有MCP服务器上的智能体基准在实时状态数据下脆弱的问题，提出DynamicMCPBench可复用框架，通过实时轨迹提炼效果检查点并评分。在24个模型、121个服务器和750个任务上，最强智能体仅解决约一半任务，31%的任务无模型解决，且工具链越长准确率越低。

<h3 id="496">[496] Masked Distillation: Internalizing the Chain-of-Thought in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.22629](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22629) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Durgesh Kalwar, Vardhan Palod, Subbarao Kambhampati
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Chain-of-Thought, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对大型推理模型的长思维链导致延迟和成本高的问题，提出掩码蒸馏框架，让学生模型仅预测解令牌，通过推理教师反馈进行蒸馏，并支持自蒸馏和双模型设置。在GSM8K和Countdown上的受控实验展示了该方法的效果。

<h3 id="497">[497] Instruction-Tuned Language Models Cannot Sample from Distributions They Can Describe</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.25292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25292) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chaemin Jang, Dongman Lee, Jihee Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Persona, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 问题：指令微调的语言模型作为人类调查受访者代理时，无法从分布中采样，而是坍缩到单一输出。方法：提出KNOWS/DOES分裂概念，利用单次调用让模型描述响应分布，并设计Prompt-Perturbed Argyle (PPA)方法。结果：相比人物聚合，单次描述将误差减半；PPA在无额外成本下将误差降低21%。

<h3 id="498">[498] Messier: A High-Resolution Corpus for Cross-Benchmark Agent Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.25891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25891) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Stefan Krsteski, Charlotte Meyer, Guillaume Allegre, Tony O&#39;Halloran, Alexandre Sallinen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: Agent Benchmark, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：AI代理跨交互环境的评估因任务、支架、验证器和评分规则碎片化而难以统一。方法：构建MESSIER语料库，包含957,611条记录、30个基准、745个代理，并标准化异构组件；引入反事实重打分。结果：前沿进展不均衡，函数调用评估饱和、编程提升最快、企业工作流最难；能力评分与Epoch指数Spearman相关系数0.84。

<h3 id="499">[499] MultivationBench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Sequential Motivation Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2607.26465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26465) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kawai Chung, Chunkit Chan, Yauwai Yim, Yuxuan Liu, Haochen Shi, Weiqi Wang, Qing Zong, Tianshi Zheng, Yixuan Fu, Kai Chung Wong, Hao Liang, Yifan Gao, Xi Yang, Janet Hui-wen Hsiao, Yangqiu Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, Social Reasoning
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HKUST-KnowComp/MultivationBench)
- **Summary**: 问题：多模态大模型在顺序动机推理上的能力尚未被充分研究，现有评估仅关注静态文本或孤立视觉快照。方法：提出MultivationBench基准，基于Maslow和Reiss心理学框架，在故事驱动的视觉叙事中评估多模态动机推理。结果：所有测试模型均难以保持跨顺序上下文的动机推理一致性，暴露了静态识别与动态推理之间的鸿沟。

<h3 id="500">[500] TaskPress: Query-Agnostic KV Cache Compression via Task-Guided Pruning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.03276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03276) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wonpyo Park, Seung-won Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 问题：长上下文推理中KV缓存随序列长度线性增长，现有剪枝方法依赖具体查询，无法跨未见查询复用。方法：提出TaskPress框架，利用任务指南作为元查询在预填充阶段过滤无关标记，并用量化尺度因子作为零成本信号检测异常值。结果：在多种长上下文任务上高效创建紧凑且可复用的缓存。

<h3 id="501">[501] Unequal Verdicts: Investigating Gender Bias in LLM-Based Fake News Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.03627](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03627) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Razieh Chalehchaleh, Reza Farahbakhsh, Noel Crespi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Bias Mitigation, Fairness, Fake News Detection
- **Venue**: 6th Workshop on Bias and Fairness in AI at ECML PKDD 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/raziehch/GenderedLIARDataset)
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM用于自动事实核查时存在性别偏见，但该问题尚未被系统研究。方法：基于LIAR基准，为每条陈述生成说话人职位的三种性别变体，评估六个LLM的偏见与公平性。结果：9.79%-35.13%的陈述获得不一致标签，男-女翻转率6.5%-23.6%；五个模型表现出显著的方向性偏见。

<h3 id="502">[502] NxN E-valuation: Hypothesis Certification via a Conformal CRT Null</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.06621](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06621) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bin Wang, Yan Zhong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Conformal Prediction, Statistical Testing
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM在假设提出中表现优异但存在幻觉，循环验证和留出测试等方法均有缺陷。方法：提出NxN E-valuation，利用大规模训练集使不同样本互为零假设，实现条件随机化检验(CRT)来认证假设。结果：在LLM生成适用于每个样本的假设条件下，可普遍替代循环验证和留出数据测试。

<h3 id="503">[503] The Optimizer Is the Agent: Reasoning-Driven Search across Prompts, Programs, and ML Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.06714](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06714) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junbo Li, Boyi Liu, Canwen Xu, Yite Wang, Yuxiong He, Zhangyang Wang, Qiang Liu, Zhewei Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agentic Optimization, LLM Agent, Automated Planning
- **Summary**: 问题：现有提示、程序和ML工作流优化系统依赖显式外层控制器，如进化搜索或bandit。方法：提出ReASearch统一框架，使单个工具使用智能体自主决定评估、诊断、编辑和验证。结果：在14个任务上优于或匹敌专用优化系统，提升2%-40%，并涌现出复杂的搜索行为。

<h3 id="504">[504] PsychoAgent: An Affect-Sensitive Cognitive Architecture for Conflict-Aware Memory in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.07438](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07438) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Amanlou, Parham Abed Azad, Farbod Davoodi, Mostafa Masumi, Behnam Bahrak, Abdol-Hossein Vahabie
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, LLM Agent, Memory Architecture
- **Venue**: BICA 2026 proceedings
- **Summary**: 问题：人类认知不仅基于主题相似性选择记忆，情感显著性和未解决冲突也影响可访问性。方法：提出PsychoAgent认知架构，分离事实与情感记忆，通过冲突感知执行控制器整合两者。结果：完整架构在冲突关键记忆检索上达到0.933，优于基线(0.500/0.667)，语义相似性代价很小。

<h3 id="505">[505] Renormalising Generative Models for Active Inference: Foundations, Derivations, and Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.09512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09512) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Karim Zaghw, Andrew Pashea, Marc Pritsch, Wouter Nuijten, Karl Friston, Lancelot Da Costa
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Active Inference, Generative Model, Probabilistic Inference
- **Venue**: 7th International Workshop on Active Inference (IWAI 2026)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/atomresearch/RGMs)
- **Summary**: 问题：主动推理框架难以扩展到丰富的空间和时间领域，且参考实现融入特定软件环境，数学细节不透明。方法：提供自包含、面向推导的规范化生成模型(RGM)说明及开放验证实现，明确层级构建、信念更新和层间信息传递。结果：澄清理论并与原始实现分离，降低入门障碍，增强可复制性和可审计性。

<h3 id="506">[506] Towards Query-Agnostic RAG Evaluation via Query Coverage and Claim Verifiability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.11238](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11238) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jeonghwan Choi, Taewon Yun, Minjeong Ban, Gyeonghun Sun, Jae-Gil Lee, Hwanjun Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Evaluation, Information Retrieval
- **Venue**: COLM 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DISL-Lab/Q-CaRE-COLM-26)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有RAG评估框架难以在多样查询上提供一致、细粒度的诊断。方法：提出Q-CARE，将查询分解为子查询、答案分解为原子声明，基于查询覆盖和声明可验证性建立统一评估原则。结果：在8个数据集的人类标注基准上，与人类判断的相关性高于RAGEval、RAGChecker等四种现有指标。

<h3 id="507">[507] Evo-Harness: Context-to-Harness Skill Compilation for Self-Evolving Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.15071](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15071) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianxin Wei, Zhan Shi, Minhua Lin, Bing He, Zewen Liu, Yisi Sang, Yuanchen Bei, Xuying Ning, Jiaru Zou, Ting-Wei Li, Xiao Lin, Yanjun Zhao, Chi Wang, Benoit Dumoulin, Dakuo Wang, Jingrui He, Hanqing Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Skill Learning, Self-Evolving Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/A-EVO-Lab/a-evolve/tree/release/evo-harness)
- **Summary**: 问题：现实环境中的代理持续遇到新任务，常只有一次改进机会，执行上下文嘈杂且混合有用与任务特定信息。方法：提出Evo-Harness，通过上下文到鞍具的技能编译(context-to-harness skill compilation)将单次执行提炼为可复用技能鞍具。结果：在五个真实基准上验证了单次技能编译的有效性，并系统分析了自我改进的关键因素。

<h3 id="508">[508] Constitutive Priors for Machine Intelligence: A Legitimacy Theory of the Artificial Physical World</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.15147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.15147) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiang Jiang, Yifu Sun, Qi Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Knowledge Representation, Formal Methods
- **Summary**: 问题：机器智能进入物理世界面临结构性鸿沟：部署需要可审计判断、故障样本稀缺、规范存在于设计文档而非运行数据。方法：提出合法性理论，划分四个世界，给出颁布准则(Promulgation Criterion)，并设计四层框架与规则引擎/LLM分工。结果：在封闭概念层上故障定位多项式时间可判定，所有判断可追溯；给出四个可证伪预测及形式化证明。

<h3 id="509">[509] Beyond the Trace: Coupling an Interpretable Reasoning-State Readout to Native MoE Routing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.17638](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17638) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kang Chen, Sihan Zhao, Yixin Cao, Yu-Gang Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 问题：推理模型写出的轨迹只是过程的部分记录，无法揭示潜在状态。方法：引入J64语义框架(从模型推理状态蒸馏)和R64代理(从专家路由统计重建)，支持测试时决策和路由编辑。结果：J64使AUC提升0.096-0.135，R64与J64中位相关0.69-0.86，保留95%-100%预测增益；停止-重采样策略提升准确率1.1-5.9点。

<h3 id="510">[510] RTPO: Reverse-Turn Policy Optimization for Stabilizing Agentic RL Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.18682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18682) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yugu Li, Zehong Cao, Jianglin Qiao, Siyi Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Agentic RL, Policy Optimization
- **Summary**: 问题：多轮代理RL训练高度不稳定，常导致性能严重退化。方法：提出Reverse-Turn Policy Optimization (RTPO)，将多轮rollout组织为稀疏反向树，按时间逆序进行轮级策略更新。结果：在agentic RL基准上相比轨迹级和轮级基线分别提升21.50%和10.76%，并提供理论保证。

<h3 id="511">[511] SAPO: Single-Rollout Autoregressive Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.19842](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19842) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dayang Liang, Lang Feng, Bo An, Yunlong Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Agentic RL, Policy Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dy-liang/SAPO)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有无评论家方法缺乏价值泛化和时间信用分配，长程任务中可能出现优势坍缩，采样预算与性能存在权衡。方法：提出SAPO，策略与价值函数共享单一自回归主干，结合轨迹级GAE和批归一化。结果：在ALFWorld和WebShop上平均超过PPO和GRPO 15.1和12.1个百分点，相比PPO减少33.2%运行时间。

<h3 id="512">[512] Is Multimodal Speculative Decoding Ready for Diffusion-Based Parallel Drafting? A Survey and Empirical Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.20743](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20743) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yantao Li, Huanlin Gao, Fang Zhao, Chao Tan, Qiang Hui, Shuting Liu, Fuyuan Shi, Ting Lu, Shaoan Zhao, Xueqiang Guo, Xinpei Su, Jianbing Zhang, Xinyu Dai, Kai Wang, Shiguo Lian
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, Multimodal Learning, Survey
- **Summary**: 问题：块并行生成式草稿(如基于扩散的方法)在多模态推测解码中的适用性仍未被探索。方法：结合以模态为中心的综述与跨架构实证研究，提出统一分类法，并在标准多模态基准上比较现有方法。结果：文本单模态可达3.6倍加速，多模态仍开放；总结了当前局限与未来方向。

<h3 id="513">[513] AUDITA: certified auditing and causal attribution of adverse outcomes in autonomous multi-agent systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.22160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22160) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhixu Du, Yiran Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Causal Attribution, AI Audit
- **Summary**: 针对自主多智能体系统中损害责任难以划分的问题，提出AUDITA审计层，结合防篡改的命令记录与认证的因果归因引擎。实验表明，在实时LM流水线上责任误差降低约3倍，且能恢复单一责任人基线无法识别的责任。

<h3 id="514">[514] ExecRubrics: Executable Tool-Augmented Rubrics for Verifiable and Efficient Long-Form Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.22559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22559) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kaustubh D. Dhole, Charles L. A. Clarke, Eugene Y. Agichtein
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Findings
- **Summary**: 针对自然语言评分标准模糊且依赖LLM评判的问题，提出ExecRubrics框架，将评分标准表示为可执行的Python评分函数。在三个基准上无需LLM法官即可接近GPT-5.5智能体基线，平均加速192倍；结合文本处理库可进一步提升准确率。

<h3 id="515">[515] Robustness Analysis of Agentic AI to Inconsistent and Incomplete Tool Responses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.22676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22676) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiachen Xu, Torben Bach Pedersen, Zhongming Yao, Xiaoyu Zhang, Yushuai Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Robustness
- **Summary**: 针对工具使用智能体面对不一致/不完整工具返回时难以诊断失败类型的问题，结合似然信号与动作信号在返回边界进行分析。结果表明似然信号能捕获不完整返回和直接不一致，动作信号可揭示失败对决策的影响，但可靠诊断需多信号融合。

<h3 id="516">[516] Characterizing Necessary Losers to Explain Tournaments Solutions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.23446](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23446) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Contet Clément, Umberto Grandi, Jérôme Mengin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Computational Social Choice, Abductive Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对锦标赛规则中解释候选者为何落选的问题，定义破坏性最小支持集作为释因解释。对六种常见规则给出了必要失败者与可能获胜者的刻画，并给出最小支持集大小及多项式算法（Borda/Copeland猜想同样多项式）。

<h3 id="517">[517] When Seeing Is Not Enough: Benchmarking Interactive Visual Grounding in LVLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.23978](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23978) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhengxiang Wang, Owen Rambow
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Grounding, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对视觉定位评估忽略交互性（初始引用不完整或模糊）的问题，提出受控的交互式视觉定位评估框架。结果显示当前LVLM远低于人类基线，无初始描述时表现最差，且置信度校准不佳。

<h3 id="518">[518] SimGuide: Typed Multi-Context User Representations for Preference-Conditioned Agent Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.24888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chirag Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Task Planning, User Modeling
- **Summary**: 针对智能体按用户偏好规划时结构化上下文价值未测的问题，提出SimBench基准和SimGuide框架，采用类型化多上下文表示和冲突仲裁。SimGuide在三模型上偏好遵循度提高0.144-0.210，移除仲裁指令性能显著下降。

<h3 id="519">[519] post-graph-rag: A PostgreSQL-Native Bi-Temporal Graph RAG Engine with Temporal Grounding at Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.24921](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24921) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chandan Rajah
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: GraphRAG, Knowledge Graph, Temporal Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/crajah/post-graph-rag;), [code](https://github.com/crajah/post-graph)
- **Summary**: 针对图RAG在基础设施、质量和时间三方面的代价问题，提出PostgreSQL原生的双时态图RAG引擎，验证抽取输出并加入时间落地。相比LightRAG图更密且边标签更简洁，在LongMemEval上准确率85.8%，时间推理从0.496提升至0.881。

<h3 id="520">[520] Towards Reliable, Generalizable, and Specific In-Context Knowledge Editing via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.25100](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25100) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuzhong Wang, Maiqi Jiang, Tejal Nair, Girija Bhusal, Yanfu Zhang, Haipeng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Knowledge Editing, Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Objective Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对上下文知识编辑中RL方法只优化单一目标且提示构建不完整的问题，提出MO-IKE，将提示构建建模为约束MDP。在Llama-3.2上编辑成功率从85.0%升至92.0%，释义一致性从77%到79%，保留率提升23.0%。

<h3 id="521">[521] Repair or Resample? Rethinking Failure Debugging in LLM Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.25920](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25920) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhongwen Luan, Xiaoyu Zhang, Ming Hu, Yue Yang, Jiongchi Yu, Xiaohong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Agent Debugging
- **Summary**: 针对LLM多智能体系统调试方法是否真正修复失败的问题，提出SymTrace评估框架和SymFail数据集。结果表明无引导重跑方法修复率仅6.90%，症状驱动干预修复率达20.15%，较SOTA提升191.89%。

<h3 id="522">[522] Candidate supply and answer selection shape the value of LLM judging in multi-agent systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.25937](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25937) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jia-Hao Ji, Sijie Li, Jiabei Cheng, Zixi She, Jin-Tai Yu, Zhiyuan Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM-as-a-Judge, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体系统已生成正确答案却上报错误的现象，将推理视为候选生成-通信-选择的流水线，研究LLM评判的选择压力。结合答案频率与评判可将准确率从63.82%提升至70.82-70.95%。

<h3 id="523">[523] ProgRouter: Online Progress-Guided Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows under Quality-Cost Tradeoffs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.25992](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25992) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Songyuan Li, Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem, Shiqiang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, LLM Routing
- **Venue**: Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 针对级联路由无法适应多步骤工作流动态度的问题，提出ProgRouter在线进度引导路由框架，通过进度评分器和元门控平衡质量与成本。在多个基准上降低运行成本并保持任务性能。

<h3 id="524">[524] LiveSim: Simulating Environment-Shaped Users in Multi-Agent Live-Stream Ecosystems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.26849](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26849) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiaqi Xu, Yiran Qiao, Jing Chen, Qiwei Zhong, Xiang Ao, Xueqi Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Social Simulation, User Simulation, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 针对LLM用户模拟依赖静态画像、忽视环境塑造的问题，提出LiveSim框架，将用户表示为可编辑行为假设并通过轨迹交互修正。在真实直播风控数据上提升了用户级行为保真度，支持生态级分析。

<h3 id="525">[525] A Multi-Modal AI Framework for Real-Time Queue Prediction, Management and Optimisation in Intelligent Border Control Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.27010](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27010) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Varvara Mama, Eleni Veroni, Nikolaos Kapsalis, Christos D. Nikolopoulos, Anargyros T. Baklezos
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Optimization, Model Predictive Control
- **Summary**: 针对边境检查队列管理依赖静态数据的问题，提出多模态AI框架，用LSTM预测队列并结合MPC和调度优化。相比ARIMA和规则方法，预测误差降低35%，平均等待时间降低30%，吞吐量提升约20%。

<h3 id="526">[526] LongGuard: Mechanistic Analysis and Training-Free Mitigation of Long-Context Failure in Safety Guardrails</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.27580](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27580) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziyang Chen, Xing Wu, Songlin Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Safety Evaluation, Long Context
- **Summary**: 针对安全护栏在长上下文下失败的问题，提出LongGuard框架，包含SafetyNIAH评估、机制分析和无训练缓解。15个护栏安全召回率平均下降超50%；CAHR-CD和CAHR-AHS分别提升平均表现22%和13%。

<h3 id="527">[527] RealSWE: A Compositional Evaluation of Coding Agents under Realistic User Requests</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.27831](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27831) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Gyuhyeong Kim, Hyojung Gwon, Jeonghyeon Kim, Kyuhong Shim, Sunjae Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Code Agents, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对SWE-bench任务与真实用户请求差异的问题，构建RealSWE基准，包含381个多变量任务族。结果表明真实输入使解决率平均降低6.4个百分点，包含期望行为和动机可显著提升性能，而环境信息无益。

<h3 id="528">[528] Rubric-to-Code Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.27906](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27906) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rui Jin, Jikai Chen, Yihan Chen, Hao Zhou, Demin Zhu, Kaichen Yang, Dong Wang, Linjian Mo, Chenyi Zhuang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Code Generation, Credit Assignment
- **Summary**: 针对GRPO将结构化结果简化为序列级奖励导致信用分配薄弱的问题，提出Rubric-to-Code信用分配(RCCA)，将rubric级反馈转化为局部优化信号。Ling-RCCA-Flash在MiniAppBench上得分41.25，在ArtifactsBench达76.19并超过GPT-5。

<h3 id="529">[529] WeAgent-MMSearch: Native Text-Vision Interaction for Multimodal Search Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.28062](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28062) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zongkai Liu, Hui Zhang, Liqiang Niu, Zhen Cao, Han Li, Juntao Liu, Wenchao Chen, Chengduo Zhao, Chao Yu, Fandong Meng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Agent, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 问题：现有智能体搜索环境通常仅以文本形式提供检索证据并忽略工具返回的图像，且长程交互中工具调用、响应长度、超时和预算失败会浪费计算并干扰策略更新。方法：提出WeAgent-Harness支持原生文本-视觉交互和运行时恢复，并在此基础上构建WeAgent-MMSearch系统，包含数据构建、基于失败感知GSPO（FA-GSPO）的智能体后训练和VisTarget-Bench基准。结果：后训练将平均得分提升19.22分，使模型超越同规模开源模型并媲美参数约为其10倍的模型。

<h3 id="530">[530] Timing-Aware Repurchase Prediction for Web-Scale E-Commerce: Survival Models for Multi-Surface Grocery Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.28393](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28393) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Akshay Kekuda, Shreeranjani Srirangamsridharan, Ishan Bhatt, Yanan Cao, Sinduja Subramaniam, Evren Korpeoglu, Kaushiki Nag, Kannan Achan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Survival Analysis, Recommender System
- **Venue**: ReSys 2026 RecTemp Workshop
- **Summary**: 问题：电商回购推荐常建模为固定时长的二分类问题，需要为每个预测时长单独训练模型。方法：用生存模型直接预测回购时间，并在大型杂货电商平台上进行广泛评估，采用加速失效时间（AFT）模型和参数校准。结果：单个AFT模型可替代三个按时段分类器且树数量减少约3倍；指数AFT校准误差（ECE）约1e-4，比Log-Normal低约10倍，同时保持排名性能。

<h3 id="531">[531] Prove2Me: An Open Collaborative Platform for Scaling Math Formalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.28433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28433) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuze Chen, Kunal Marwaha, Xiaoyang Lu, Henry Yuen, Tianyi Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: 问题：数学形式化项目面临专业知识和时间成本高的进入壁垒。方法：提出Prove2Me开放协作平台，用户发起形式化"任务"，AI智能体协作完成Lean形式证明，并设计机制和专用框架支持大规模协作。结果：平台旨在将数学形式化转变为可扩展的众包努力，目前无定量指标。

<h3 id="532">[532] Logos: An Agent Harness on a Cross-Process Bus</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2608.28553](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28553) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hanzhang Jia, Liheng Zeng, Hao Cheng, Yi Gao, Bo Ma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Agent Harness, Fault Tolerance
- **Summary**: 问题：现有智能体系统通常将插件放在单一进程和共享上下文中，导致所有组件处于同一物理故障域，一次故障中断所有会话。方法：提出Logos，一个基于跨进程总线的类ROS智能体框架，插件作为独立进程，唯一共享状态为追加式转录。结果：在工具调用周期四个边界处的kill后，80个会话可无重复效果地恢复；同故障对比中，单进程配置一次故障中断所有共驻会话，而对等进程配置中故障仅终止于一个节点。

<h3 id="533">[533] Simulation-Based Evaluation of Energy-Constrained Quantum-Classical Competition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2308.08025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.08025) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junyu Liu, Hansheng Jiang, Zuo-Jun Max Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Energy Management, Game Theory
- **Summary**: 问题：评估量子与经典计算公司在有限能源资源下的市场竞争的能源影响。方法：构建基于仿真的框架，将厂商建模为差异化古诺竞争者，服务容量由技术特定的能源缩放规律决定，并刻画容量约束的纳什均衡。结果：证明存在需求规模使得量子服务更节能，并在离子阱和里德伯平台上进行数值实验，指出量子能源优势何时仅为渐近、何时具有操作相关性。

<h3 id="534">[534] General Phrase Debiaser: Debiasing Masked Language Models at a Multi-Token Level</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2311.13892](https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.13892) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bingkang Shi, Xiaodan Zhang, Dehan Kong, Yulei Wu, Zongzhen Liu, Honglei Lyu, Longtao Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Bias Mitigation, Masked Language Modeling
- **Venue**: ICASSP 2024
- **Summary**: 问题：掩码语言模型中的偏见主要在词级被处理，短语级偏见关注不足。方法：提出General Phrase Debiaser自动多词元去偏流程，包括短语过滤阶段和模型去偏阶段，在词元级处理短语偏见。结果：在标准数据集和指标上达到最先进结果，显著降低职业和多个学科中的性别偏见，且适用于不同参数规模的模型。

<h3 id="535">[535] SUB-PLAY: Adversarial Policies against Partially Observed Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2402.03741](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03741) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Oubo Ma, Yuwen Pu, Linkang Du, Yang Dai, Ruo Wang, Xiaolei Liu, Yingcai Wu, Shouling Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Partially Observable RL
- **Venue**: ACM Conference on Computer and Communications Security (CCS'24)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有针对多智能体强化学习的对抗策略攻击通常假设攻击者具有完全全局观测，部分可观测下攻击能力未知。方法：提出黑盒攻击SUB-PLAY，通过构建多个子博弈并共享子策略转移来缓解部分可观测性的影响。结果：在三种典型部分可观测限制下验证有效性，可视化显示对抗策略显著改变受害者策略网络激活，并评估了三种防御。

<h3 id="536">[536] PQMass: Probabilistic Assessment of the Quality of Generative Models using Probability Mass Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2402.04355](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04355) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pablo Lemos, Sammy Sharief, Esmeralda S. Whitammer, Salma Salhi, Connor Stone, Laurence Perreault-Levasseur, Yashar Hezaveh
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ME
- **Tags**: Generative Model Evaluation, Statistical Testing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Ciela-Institute/PQM)
- **Summary**: 问题：评估生成模型质量需要似然或辅助模型，难以直接比较分布。方法：提出PQMass，一种无似然方法，将样本空间划分为非重叠区域并用卡方检验比较两个样本集的区域计数，给出p值。结果：在多种模态和维度上验证其评估质量、新颖性和多样性的有效性，并扩展到中等高维数据。

<h3 id="537">[537] Gender, Race, and Intersectional Bias in Resume Screening via Language Model Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.20371](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.20371) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kyra Wilson, Aylin Caliskan
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Fairness, Gender Bias
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/kyrawilson/Resume-Screening-Bias)
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM在简历筛选应用中可能因保护属性产生偏见。方法：通过文档检索框架模拟职位候选人筛选，对大量开源简历和职位描述进行简历审计，评估多款MTE模型。结果：MTE在85.1%的情况下显著偏好白人关联名字，仅11.1%偏好女性名字；黑人男性在多达100%的情况下处于劣势，验证了三个交叉性假设。

<h3 id="538">[538] Understanding Deep Learning via Notions of Rank</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2408.02111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02111) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Noam Razin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 问题：深度学习的形式化理解有限，特别是泛化和表达能力。方法：提出用秩的概念理解深度学习，建立梯度训练可诱发低秩隐式正则化，并通过张量分解连接神经网络，用秩刻画图神经网络的交互建模能力。结果：在音频、图像和文本等自然数据上实证表明该现象有助于解释泛化，并为显式正则化和数据预处理提供实用方案。

<h3 id="539">[539] Learning Personalized Prompts for Healthcare Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2412.15957](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15957) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruize Shi, Hong Huang, Wei Zhou, Kehan Yin, Kai Zhao, Yun Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Prompt Engineering, Medical AI
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CGCL-codes/PPL)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有LLM医疗建议依赖通用医学知识，忽略个体差异。方法：提出个性化提示学习（PPL）框架，利用患者自身信息和临床相似病例的同伴信息构建初始提示，并用强化学习优化以对齐医生建议。结果：在妇产科真实数据上，PPL在专家评估中100次比较中胜出97次，生成更个性化的医疗指导。

<h3 id="540">[540] Man Made Language Models? Evaluating LLMs' Perpetuation of Masculine Generics Bias</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.10577](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10577) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Enzo Doyen, Amalia Todirascu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Gender Bias, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM在无上下文约束的通用指令下也可能延续男性通用词（MG）偏见，但研究不足。方法：构建16000+人类名词数据库，评估六个LLM在法语四种指令-响应对应数据集上的MG偏见率和性别公平语言使用。结果：约27.57%对MG过滤指令的响应存在MG偏见，含MG提示时高达78.55%；且LLM很少自发使用性别公平语言。

<h3 id="541">[541] An Efficient Sparse Fine-Tuning with Low Quantization Error via Neural Network Pruning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.11439](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11439) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cen-Jhih Li, Aditya Bhaskara
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Model Compression, Pruning
- **Venue**: Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CenjhihLi/sparsity_finetuning)
- **Summary**: 问题：微调基础模型需要大量内存和计算。方法：基于神经网络剪枝思想，用结构剪枝的特征重要性指标识别重要神经元，并仅更新相关权重进行稀疏微调。结果：在常见语言任务上将稀疏微调内存效率提高20-50%，同时匹配LoRA变体等最先进方法的准确率。

<h3 id="542">[542] CLIPure: Purification in Latent Space via CLIP for Adversarially Robust Zero-Shot Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.18176](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18176) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mingkun Zhang, Keping Bi, Wei Chen, Jiafeng Guo, Xueqi Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Vision-Language Model, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Venue**: ICLR 2025
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TMLResearchGroup-CAS/CLIPure)
- **Summary**: 问题：零样本分类器需要对抗鲁棒性，但现有防御依赖特定攻击类型的对抗训练。方法：提出CLIPure，在CLIP多模态潜空间中进行净化，基于双向SDE推导净化风险，并给出Diff和Cos两种变体；Cos变体不基于生成模型，大幅提升防御效率。结果：在CIFAR10上鲁棒性从71.7%提升至91.1%，ImageNet从59.6%提升至72.6%，13个数据集上的平均鲁棒性相对提升108%。

<h3 id="543">[543] RSPO: Regularized Self-Play Alignment of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.00030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00030) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaohang Tang, Sangwoong Yoon, Seongho Son, Huizhuo Yuan, Quanquan Gu, Ilija Bogunovic
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Self-Play, Preference Optimization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xiaohangt/RSPO)
- **Summary**: 问题：自博弈策略优化在LLM对齐中有效，但对参考策略的正则化研究不足。方法：提出RSPO框架，统一先验方法，支持即插即用正则器，并保持对应正则博弈的纳什均衡收敛。结果：在AlpacaEval-2上，RSPO将无正则自博弈基线（SPPO）的LCWR从Mistral-7B的28.5%提升至35.4%，LLaMA-8B从38.77%提升至43.66%，Gemma-2B从50.54%提升至51.83%。

<h3 id="544">[544] Multimodal Large Language Models Predict Urban Safety Perception but Encode Non-Neutral Demographic Priors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.00610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00610) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ciro Beneduce, Bruno Lepri, Massimiliano Luca
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal LLM, Bias Detection, Urban Computing
- **Summary**: 问题：传统城市安全感知调查成本高且难扩展，MLLM能否从街景图像评估安全感知并考虑观察者差异。方法：使用Place Pulse 2.0评估四个MLLM，在中性提示和人口统计学角色（性别、年龄、种族）下分类安全，并分析关键词。结果：所有模型零样本宏F1为65-69%，但系统性地偏向安全类、低估不安全并压缩城市间差异；女性角色比男性角色产生更多不安全分类，种族影响因模型而异。

<h3 id="545">[545] HeTGB: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Heterophilic Text-Attributed Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.04822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04822) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shujie Li, Yuxia Wu, Yuan Fang, Chuan Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Heterophilous Learning, Benchmark, Text-Attributed Graph
- **Summary**: 现有图神经网络多基于同质性假设，且缺乏同时包含异质性结构和丰富文本属性的基准。为此提出HeTGB基准，包含五个来自不同领域的真实异质性文本属性图数据集，并在节点分类任务上系统评估了GNN、预训练语言模型和协同训练方法。实验展示了文本属性在异质性图中的价值，并揭示了现有模型的局限。

<h3 id="546">[546] A Comprehensive Survey on Multi-Agent Cooperative Decision-Making: Scenarios, Approaches, Challenges and Perspectives</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.13415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.13415) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Weiqiang Jin, Hongyang Du, Shixiang Tang, Biao Zhao, Guang Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Survey
- **Summary**: 多智能体协同决策在众多领域有广泛应用，但缺乏系统性综述。本文全面调研了主流仿真环境与平台，并从规则、博弈论、进化算法、深度多智能体强化学习和LLM推理五个类别梳理了决策方法，重点分析MARL和LLM方法的优劣。最后讨论了未来研究方向与挑战。

<h3 id="547">[547] Towards Accurate and Lightweight Peripheral Neuroblastic Tumor Diagnosis via Contrastive Multi-scale Pathological Image Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.13754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.13754) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhu Zhu, Shuo Jiang, Jingyuan Zheng, Yawen Li, Yifei Chen, Manli Zhao, Weizhong Gu, Feiwei Qin, Jinhu Wang, Gang Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Histopathology, Kolmogorov-Arnold Network
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/JSLiam94/CoPath)
