- **Summary**: 外周神经母细胞瘤的病理亚型分类面临队列有限、异质性强和计算开销大等挑战。提出CoPath框架，包含轻量级多尺度特征融合网络CoHisNet（利用Kolmogorov-Arnold网络层增强非线性建模）和基于病理先验的聚合模块PathVote。在私有队列和BreakHis数据集上，该方法以更低的计算复杂度取得了有竞争力或更优的性能。

<h3 id="548">[548] ReGraP-LLaVA: Reasoning enabled Graph-based Personalized Large Language and Vision Assistant</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.03654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.03654) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yifan Xiang, Zhenxi Zhang, Bin Li, Yixuan Weng, Bo Gao, Shoujun Zhou, Yangfan He, Yilin Yuan, Keqin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Knowledge Graph, Graph Prompt Learning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xyfyyds/ReGraP)
- **Summary**: 现有个性化多模态大模型主要关注孤立概念，缺乏关系建模和评估。为此构建ReGraP数据集（120个个性化知识集，含图像、知识图谱和思维链问答对），并提出ReGraP-LLaVA模型，通过软/硬图提示将结构化关系知识融入模型。在ReGraP基准上，该方法在各类题型中取得最佳整体性能。

<h3 id="549">[549] Watch your steps: Dormant Adversarial Behaviors that Activate upon LLM Finetuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.16567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16567) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thibaud Gloaguen, Mark Vero, Robin Staab, Martin Vechev
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Backdoor Attack, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 现有研究认为在良性数据上微调是安全的，但本文首次证明攻击者可通过元学习构造看似正常、一旦被下游用户微调即触发对抗行为的LLM。所提FAB攻击在保持模型常规能力的同时，使微调后出现如垃圾广告、越狱和过度拒绝等目标行为。实验验证了该方法在多种LLM和训练配置下的有效性与鲁棒性。

<h3 id="550">[550] Learning Composable Chains-of-Thought</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.22635](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22635) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fangcong Yin, Zeyu Leo Liu, Liu Leqi, Xi Ye, Greg Durrett
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Chain-of-Thought, Compositional Generalization, Model Merging
- **Summary**: 针对缺乏目标组合任务标注数据的问题，提出通过设计可组合的原子任务思维链（Composable CoT）格式，训练原子CoT模型，并利用多任务学习或模型合并实现组合泛化。在字符串操作和自然语言技能组合任务上，该方法在给定数据预算下优于多任务学习和继续微调基线。

<h3 id="551">[551] EquiReg: Equivariance Regularized Diffusion for Inverse Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.22973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.22973) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bahareh Tolooshams, Aditi Chandrashekar, Rayhan Zirvi, Abbas Mammadov, Jiachen Yao, Chuwei Wang, Anima Anandkumar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Inverse Problem, Equivariant Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Anima-Lab/EquiReg)
- **Summary**: 扩散模型求解逆问题时常用各向同性高斯近似似然，易导致估计偏离数据流形。提出EquiReg插件框架，利用等变性误差作为正则项，引导采样轨迹停留在流形上，从而提升重建质量并加速收敛。在线性和非线性图像恢复及偏微分方程求解任务中均取得一致性能提升。

<h3 id="552">[552] mRNA Design and Optimization with Deep Knowledge-Infused Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.23862](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.23862) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zheng Gong, Ziyi Jiang, Weihao Gao, Yuanyuan Wang, Zhining Cai, Deng Zhuo, Lan Ma
- **Subjects**: q-bio.QM; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RNA Design, Molecular Generation, Knowledge Injection
- **Summary**: 现有mRNA优化方法在序列保真度、多目标优化和计算效率之间难以兼得。提出RNop，一种知识注入的Transformer，通过机制对齐的损失函数显式编码生物学先验。在超过600万序列上训练后，RNop实现绝对序列保真和生物学指标提升，体外实验表达量最高提升2.28倍，并具有机制级可解释性。

<h3 id="553">[553] Understanding Automated Program Repair Agents Through the Lens of Traceability: An Empirical Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.08311](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08311) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ira Ceka, Hailie Mitchell, Saurabh Pujar, Luca Buratti, Shyam Ramji, Junfeng Yang, Gail Kaiser, Baishakhi Ray
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Program Repair, LLM Agent, Empirical Analysis
- **Venue**: ISSTA '26
- **Summary**: 尽管自动程序修复（APR）智能体在SWE-bench等榜单上表现优异，但其决策过程和不失败模式尚未被系统研究。本文对5种APR智能体在500个真实修复任务中的完整流程进行了追踪分析，发现它们擅长简单修复，但难以处理逻辑密集型缺陷，且测试生成与回归测试选择是主要瓶颈。研究还指出当前智能体缺乏调试器或程序分析工具等问题。

<h3 id="554">[554] Evidence-Grounded Trustworthy Multimodal Reasoning and Evaluation Benchmark in Complex Urban Scenes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.09557](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09557) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhaoyang Wei, Bowen Jiang, Xumeng Han, Jiashu Li, Xuehui Yu, Yuling Liu, Guorong Li, Zhenjun Han, Jianbin Jiao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: IJCV
- **Summary**: 多模态大模型在复杂城市场景中的推理常缺乏视觉证据支撑，而现有基准只评估最终结果。作者提出AD2-Bench，引入层级视觉诊断框架以分解推理过程，并进一步提出EGVOR方法，通过生成结构化证据原子（空间-语义三元组）显式对齐定位与语义信息，并结合课程强化学习训练。实验表明EGVOR显著提升不利条件下的推理稳定性。

<h3 id="555">[555] FAA Framework: A Large Language Model-Based Approach for Credit Card Fraud Investigations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.11635](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.11635) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shaun Shuster, Eyal Zloof, Asaf Shabtai, Rami Puzis
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fraud Detection, LLM Agent, Financial AI
- **Summary**: 信用卡欺诈调查中分析师面临大量告警，导致告警疲劳。提出首个欺诈调查助手FIA，利用多模态大语言模型自动完成证据收集、推理和报告生成。在Sparkov和CCTD数据集上，FIA在处理边界案例时逐步提升F1分数，经过1,500次额外调查后提升8%。

<h3 id="556">[556] Federated Learning for MRI-based BrainAGE: a multicenter study on post-stroke functional outcome prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.15626](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15626) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vincent Roca, Marc Tommasi, Paul Andrey, Aurélien Bellet, Markus D. Schirmer, Hilde Henon, Laurent Puy, Julien Ramon, Grégory Kuchcinski, Martin Bretzner, Renaud Lopes
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Medical AI, Neuroimaging
- **Summary**: 训练脑龄预测（BrainAGE）模型需要大规模数据，但隐私限制阻碍数据集中。本研究基于16个中心的1674例卒中患者FLAIR影像，比较了集中式、联邦学习和单中心学习三种策略。结果表明联邦学习优于单中心模型，且BrainAGE与血管危险因素及三个月功能预后显著相关，显示其在卒中预后建模中的潜力。

<h3 id="557">[557] Training-free LLM Verification via Recycling Few-shot Examples</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.17251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17251) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dongseok Lee, Jimyung Hong, Dongyoung Kim, Jaehyung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, In-Context Learning, Few-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM输出随机性且现有验证方法需额外训练的问题，提出ReFeri框架，复用少样本示例同时用于生成和验证候选输出。通过结合前向置信度与后向重建惩罚来选择遵循少样本指导且避免过拟合的答案。在三个LLM和七个任务上，该方法平均相对准确率提升8.2%。

<h3 id="558">[558] A foundation model with multi-variate parallel attention to generate neuronal activity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.20354](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20354) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Francesco Carzaniga, Michael Hersche, Abu Sebastian, Kaspar Schindler, Abbas Rahimi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Neuroscience, Time Series
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/IBM/multi-variate-parallel-transformer)
- **Summary**: 颅内脑电（iEEG）等多元时间序列的通道配置高度异质，给建模带来挑战。提出多变量并行注意力（MVPA），解耦内容、时间和空间注意力，并构建生成式基础模型MVPFormer，同时发布迄今最大的公开iEEG数据集SWEC。MVPFormer在癫痫检测和四个脑电解码任务上超越现有SOTA Transformer基线。

<h3 id="559">[559] Disappearing Ink: Obfuscation Breaks N-gram Code Watermarks in Theory and Practice</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.05512](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.05512) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Gehao Zhang, Mingzhe Li, Eugene Bagdasarian, Shiqing Ma, Juan Zhai
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Watermarking, Code Obfuscation
- **Summary**: 现有N-gram代码水印的鲁棒性仅针对简单编辑评估，本文指出代码混淆可破坏这些水印。作者将语义保持变换建模为马尔可夫随机游走，证明在分布一致性假设下，混淆后水印检测失败率趋向于1-fpr。在多种方案、LLM和混淆器上验证，检测AUROC跌至接近0.5，表明当前N-gram水印无法抵抗实际混淆攻击。

<h3 id="560">[560] Proof2Hybrid: Automatic Mathematical Benchmark Synthesis for Proof-Centric Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.02208](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02208) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yebo Peng, Yaoming Li, Zixiang Liu, Zhizhuo Yang, Xinye Xu, Bowen Ye, Weijun Yuan, Zihan Wang, Tong Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Benchmark, Synthetic Data Generation
- **Summary**: 手工创建以证明为核心的数学基准成本高昂，导致LLM数学能力评估不足。提出Proof2Hybrid，首个从自然语言数学语料自动合成高质量证明类基准的框架，并设计“n选m多重判官”混合题型以抵抗猜测。基于该框架构建的代数几何基准AlgGeoTest包含456道题目，评测揭示现有LLM在代数几何理解上的显著缺陷。

<h3 id="561">[561] StructSynth: Dependency Graphs as Generation Plans for Low-Data Tabular Synthesis with Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.02601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.02601) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Siyi Liu, Yujia Zheng, Haoyang Li, Yongqi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Synthesis, Tabular Learning
- **Summary**: 现有方法在样本稀缺时难以保持表格数据特征间依赖关系。StructSynth将依赖图作为生成计划，利用LLM推理和统计线索构建有向无环图，并按其拓扑顺序指导自回归合成。实验表明，在低数据设置下，StructSynth在14个对比生成器中取得最佳下游效用和隐私风险排名。

<h3 id="562">[562] SonicMaster: Towards Controllable All-in-One Music Restoration and Mastering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.03448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03448) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jan Melechovsky, Ambuj Mehrish, Abhinaba Roy, Dorien Herremans
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.MM; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Audio Editing, Music Restoration
- **Summary**: 音乐录音常伴有混响、失真、削波等问题，通常需要多种专用工具处理。SonicMaster是一种统一的生成式音乐修复与母带处理模型，基于流匹配生成范式，通过自然语言指令或自动模式对多种伪影进行修复。客观指标显示其在所有伪影类别上显著提升音质，主观听感测试也优于基线。

<h3 id="563">[563] From Isolation to Alignment: Unified LoRA for Efficient Multi-Task Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.05078](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.05078) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jinda Liu, Yi Chang, Yuan Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Multi-Task Learning, Low-Rank Adaptation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jinda-liu/Align-LoRA)
- **Summary**: 多任务PEFT中的复杂LoRA变体依赖架构隔离任务知识，导致推理延迟。Align-LoRA提出统一单适配器LoRA，通过显式对齐损失学习任务共享表示，并可权重合并实现零推理延迟。实验证明其显著优于现有方法，且理论分析支持其有效性。

<h3 id="564">[564] MM-BrowseComp: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multimodal Browsing Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.13186](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13186) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shilong Li, Xingyuan Bu, Wenjie Wang, Jiaheng Liu, Jun Dong, Haoyang He, Hao Lu, Haozhe Zhang, Chenchen Jing, Zhen Li, Chuanhao Li, Jiayi Tian, Chenchen Zhang, Tianhao Peng, Yancheng He, Jihao Gu, Hui Huang, Donghao Zhou, Yuanxing Zhang, Jian Yang, Ge Zhang, Wenhao Huang, Zhaoxiang Zhang, Qiangpeng Yang, Shilei Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Agent, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MMBrowseComp/MM-BrowseComp)
- **Summary**: 现有网页浏览基准如BrowseComp主要关注文本，忽略多模态内容。MM-BrowseComp包含400个手工设计问题，要求从网页图文视频中提取证据，并提供核对清单以细粒度分析多模态依赖和推理路径。对27个模型的评估显示，最强的GPT-5-High仅达到24.25%准确率，说明多模态浏览能力仍待提升。

<h3 id="565">[565] EEGDM: Learning EEG Representation with Latent Diffusion Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.20705](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20705) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shaocong Wang, Tong Liu, Yihan Li, Ming Li, Kairui Wen, Pei Yang, Wenqi Ji, Minjing Yu, Yong-Jin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, EEG Analysis, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 现有EEG自监督表示学习多依赖掩码重建，难以捕获全局生成约束。EEGDM利用潜在扩散模型生成EEG信号作为目标，将编码器提取的紧凑表示作为条件引导扩散去噪，从而联合优化。实验表明EEGDM能重建高质量信号、学习鲁棒表示，并在多个下游任务上表现具竞争力。

<h3 id="566">[566] Turning the Spell Around: Lightweight Alignment Amplification via Rank-One Safety Injection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.20766](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.20766) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Harethah Abu Shairah, Hasan Abed Al Kader Hammoud, George Turkiyyah, Bernard Ghanem
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Safety Alignment, LLM Safety, Activation Steering
- **Summary**: LLM的安全对齐可被移除特定表示方向所绕过，而现有微调方法资源开销大。ROSI通过秩一权重修改将激活永久推向拒绝中介子空间，无需微调即可增强安全对齐。实验显示ROSI稳定提高Llama Guard 3评估的安全拒绝率，同时保持MMLU、HellaSwag、Arc等基准上的效用。

<h3 id="567">[567] NeuroBreak: Unveil Internal Jailbreak Mechanisms in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.03985](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.03985) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chuhan Zhang, Ye Zhang, Bowen Shi, Yuyou Gan, Tianyu Du, Shouling Ji, Dazhen Deng, Yingcai Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Attack, Visual Analytics, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Venue**: IEEE VIS 2026
- **Summary**: LLM参数空间巨大，诊断越狱机制极为困难。NeuroBreak是一个视觉分析系统，通过逐层探针和神经元级行为表征，结合多种可视化设计，帮助专家从层语义到神经元行为逐步解析越狱机制。定量评估和案例研究表明其能揭示安全失败原因并给出防御改进建议。

<h3 id="568">[568] MAGneT: Coordinated Multi-Agent Generation of Synthetic Multi-Turn Mental Health Counseling Sessions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.04183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.04183) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aishik Mandal, Tanmoy Chakraborty, Iryna Gurevych
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Data Synthesis, Mental Health
- **Summary**: 高质量心理咨询数据稀缺，现有单代理生成方法难以捕捉真实咨询的结构与细节。MAGneT采用多代理框架，将咨询师回应生成分解为由专门LLM代理处理的心理技术子任务，并构建统一评估框架。专家在9个维度上平均77.2%偏好MAGneT，且用其数据微调的模型在CTRS上平均提升6.9%。

<h3 id="569">[569] Transformer-Encoder Trees for Efficient Multilingual Machine Translation and Speech Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.17930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.17930) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiwen Guan, Jacob Whitehill
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Speech Translation, Multilingual Learning
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 多语言翻译存在计算冗余，且低资源语言质量受损。Transformer Encoder Tree (TET)是一种基于CTC的层次化非自回归编码器架构，在相近语言间共享中间表示，单次前向即可并行生成所有目标语言。相比朴素一对多设计，参数量减少66%，推理计算降低60%，语音翻译加速约7-14倍且质量接近自回归系统。

<h3 id="570">[570] Talk in Pieces, See in Whole: Disentangled and Hierarchical Representation Learning in Language-based Object Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.24192](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24192) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sojung An, Kwanyong Park, Yong Jae Lee, Donghyun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Object Detection, Compositional Reasoning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 视觉-语言模型在处理包含属性和关系从句的复杂查询时表现不佳。TaSe框架将文本标记解耦为对象、属性和关系成分，并通过层次目标聚合成句子级表示，同时构建层次化合成描述数据集。在OmniLabel基准上取得24%的性能提升，展示了语言组合性的重要性。

<h3 id="571">[571] Operationalising AI Regulatory Sandboxes: Activities, Requirements, and Technical Assessment under the EU AI Act</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.25256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25256) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alessio Buscemi, Thibault Simonetto, Daniele Pagani, German Castignani, Maxime Cordy, Jordi Cabot
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Governance, AI Policy
- **Summary**: AI系统评估在高风险领域至关重要，但现有方法碎片化、测试缺乏标准化。该文将欧盟AI监管沙箱生命周期操作化为29项活动，区分核心与扩展沙箱，并从AI法案第9-15条推导出15项基础与治理要求，提出Sandbox Configurator开源框架。该框架为监管机构、技术专家和AI提供商提供了结构化路径。

<h3 id="572">[572] When LLMs Benchmark Themselves: Deconstructing Self-Bias in Automated Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.26600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26600) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenda Xu, Sweta Agrawal, Vilém Zouhar, Markus Freitag, Daniel Deutsch
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Bias Mitigation
- **Summary**: LLM生成的基准会系统性地偏向生成它的模型。该文以机器翻译为主验证了自偏见的两个来源——测试集生成和评估器，且两者组合会放大效应。即使增加源文本多样性可部分缓解，自偏见仍足以使每个模型自我排名第一，该现象也扩展至开放生成任务。

<h3 id="573">[573] Breaking the Code: Security Assessment of AI Code Agents Through Systematic Jailbreaking Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.01359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01359) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shoumik Saha, Jifan Chen, Sam Mayers, Sanjay Krishna Gouda, Zijian Wang, Varun Kumar
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 具备代码执行能力的LLM代理使越狱后果超出纯文本场景，现有评估多关注拒绝或有害文本。JAWS-Bench包含三种工作区场景的基准，配合可执行感知的层次化法官框架评估合规性、攻击成功、语法和可执行性。结果显示，提示攻击在空工作区达到61%合规率，27%端到端可运行；包裹成代理后攻击成功率提升1.6倍。

<h3 id="574">[574] Correctness Forensics for Batch Speculative Decoding: Diagnosing the Ragged Tensor Problem</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.22876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.22876) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ranran Haoran Zhang, Soumik Dey, Ashirbad Mishra, Hansi Wu, Binbin Li, Rui Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference, Parallel Decoding
- **Venue**: Findings of the Association for Computational Linguistics: EMNLP 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/eBay/spec_dec)
- **Summary**: 批处理推测解码的多种实现会在报告竞争力的同时静默产生损坏输出。该文通过取证分析将根因归结为不规则张量问题，形式化了矩形对齐和位置ID连续等同步不变量，并提出EQSPEC和EXSPEC来维护这些不变量。EXSPEC在批大小8时达到3倍吞吐量，与标准解码的精确匹配达95%，残余差异源于浮点非确定性。

<h3 id="575">[575] Personalized Treatment Outcome Prediction from Scarce Data via Dual-Channel Knowledge Distillation and Adaptive Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.26444](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26444) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenjie Chen, Li Zhuang, Ziying Luo, Yu Liu, Jiahao Wu, Shengcai Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Clinical Prediction, Medical AI
- **Venue**: IEEE TETCI
- **Summary**: 小样本和罕见患者群体的试验数据稀缺限制了个性化治疗结局预测。CFKD-AFN通过双通道知识蒸馏从低保真模拟数据中提取互补知识，并利用注意力引导融合模块自适应整合多源信息。在COPD数据集上，其均方误差降低6.67%-74.55%，平均绝对百分比误差降低1.43%-51.54%，且对数据规模变化稳健。

<h3 id="576">[576] LeMat-Synth: a multi-modal toolbox to curate broad synthesis procedure databases from scientific literature</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.26824](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.26824) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Magdalena Lederbauer, Siddharth Betala, Valerie Gentzke, Anamaria Leonescu, Amine Sehaba, Faris Flaifil, Ayush Jain, Alfonso Amayuelas, Nikhil Yelamarthy, Xiyao Li, Grégoire Germain, Stefano Ribes, Stefan P. Schmid, Alexandre Nozadze, Anna Kelmanson, Sudheesh Kumar Ethirajan, Mohd Zaki, Elton Pan, Georgia Channing, Connor W. Coley, Philippe Schwaller, Rocío Mercado, Alexandre Duval, Mathilde L. D. Franckel, Samuel P. Gleason
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Information Extraction, Material Discovery, Dataset
- **Summary**: 材料合成流程知识散落在非结构化的文献中，难以系统分析。LeMat-Synth Parser是一个模块化开源多模态工具箱，利用LLM和VLM自动提取文本和图表中的合成协议与性能指标，并构建了含58K条合成流程的LeMat-Synth数据集。验证表明其提取质量良好，并能扩展到催化剂性能关联和超导临界温度恢复等应用。

<h3 id="577">[577] Retrofitters, pragmatists and activists: Public interest litigation for accountable automated decision-making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.03211](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03211) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Henry L Fraser, Zahra Stardust
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Accountability, AI Governance
- **Venue**: Law and Society Review
- **Summary**: 该论文研究澳大利亚公益诉讼在AI自动决策问责中的作用，指出ADM监管面临政治阻力时需依赖现有法律执行。通过访谈揭示"改造旧法"等诉讼策略及其局限，并提出支撑有效诉讼的制度需求。

<h3 id="578">[578] Uncertainty Makes It Stable: Curiosity-Driven Quantized Mixture-of-Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.11743](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.11743) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez, Luis Fernando Torres Torres, Mackenzie J. Meni, Carlos Andrés Duran Paredes, Eric Arazo, Cristian Bosch, Ricardo Simon Carbajo, Yuan Lai, Leo Anthony Celi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Quantization, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 针对资源受限设备上量化与延迟的矛盾，提出基于贝叶斯认知不确定性路由的量化MoE框架。在音频分类基准上，4-bit量化保持99.9%的F1（0.858 vs 0.859），压缩4倍、能耗降低31%，并将Quinn上F1从0.802提至0.809且方差降低85%。

<h3 id="579">[579] Error-Driven Scene Editing for 3D Grounding in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.14086](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.14086) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yue Zhang, Zun Wang, Han Lin, Jialu Li, Jianing Yang, Yonatan Bitton, Idan Szpektor, Mohit Bansal
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Visual Grounding, Scene Editing
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zhangyuejoslin/Deer-3D)
- **Summary**: 针对3D-LLM在语言与空间接地上的偏差，提出错误驱动的3D场景编辑框架DEER-3D，通过"分解-诊断-编辑-重训"循环生成反事实训练样本。在多个3D接地基准上一致提升4-6%。

<h3 id="580">[580] MedVision: Benchmarking Quantitative Medical Image Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.18676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18676) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yongcheng Yao, Yongshuo Zong, Raman Dutt, Yongxin Yang, Sotirios A Tsaftaris, Timothy Hospedales
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 针对医学视觉语言模型缺乏定量分析能力，构建大规模基准MedVision，涵盖22个数据集、29K 3D图像和24.3M注释，聚焦检测、肿瘤大小和角度/距离测量。实验显示现有VLM表现差，而基于监督和强化微调的MedVision-V0成为强基线。

<h3 id="581">[581] Look It Up: Analysing Internal Web Search Capabilities of Modern LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.18931](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.18931) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sahil Kale
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Evaluation, Web Search
- **Summary**: 评估了闭源LLM内置web检索的有效性。静态集上检索提升准确率但恶化置信度校准；动态集准确率低于70%，失败主要源于查询构造和来源选择。表明检索作为低延迟验证有效，但作为IR管道不可靠。

<h3 id="582">[582] On the Optimality of Kinship Naming: an Information-theoretic Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.19120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19120) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Phong Le, Mees Lindeman, Raquel G. Alhama
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Emergent Communication, Information Theory
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 研究亲属称谓命名系统在信息量与复杂度之间的权衡，突破先前假设，收集四种语言数据并分析不同沟通需求和听者模型的影响。通过指称博弈显示最优性在理论上可及并能在经验中涌现。

<h3 id="583">[583] WaymoQA: A Multi-View Visual Question Answering Dataset for Safety-Critical Reasoning in Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.20022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20022) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Seungjun Yu, Seonho Lee, Namho Kim, Jaeyo Shin, Junsung Park, Wonjeong Ryu, Raehyuk Jung, Hyunjung Shim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Visual Question Answering, Visual Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sjyu001/WaymoQA)
- **Summary**: 提出安全关键推理任务和多视图VQA数据集WaymoQA（35K QA对），覆盖复杂高风险驾驶场景。实验表明现有MLLM在安全关键场景表现不佳，用WaymoQA微调显著提升推理能力。

<h3 id="584">[584] CoFiRec: Coarse-to-Fine Tokenization for Generative Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.22707](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.22707) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianxin Wei, Xuying Ning, Xuxing Chen, Ruizhong Qiu, Yupeng Hou, Yan Xie, Shuang Yang, Zhigang Hua, Jingrui He
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Tokenization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YennNing/CoFiRec)
- **Summary**: 针对生成式推荐忽视用户偏好由粗到细的演化，提出CoFiRec框架，将物品语义分层独立分词并按粗到细生成。在多个基准上超越现有方法，并从理论上证明结构化分词的优越性。

<h3 id="585">[585] ScalePRM: Training Process Reward Models by Scaling Verification Compute Without Ground Truth</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.03244](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03244) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Salman Rahman, Sruthi Gorantla, Arpit Gupta, Swastik Roy, Nanyun Peng, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Process Reward Model, RLVR, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对过程奖励模型依赖昂贵人工或地面真值标签，提出ScalePRM通过扩展验证计算生成合成步级标签。ProcessBench上F1 67.5超过地面真值引导（66.4）和GPT-4o评论（61.9），RL训练准确率47.4% vs 43.9%。

<h3 id="586">[586] Better World Models Can Lead to Better Post-Training Performance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.03400](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03400) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Prakhar Gupta, Henry Conklin, Sarah-Jane Leslie, Andrew Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 研究显式世界建模目标对Transformer表示和后训练性能的影响。以魔方为域，发现显式世界建模带来更高探测准确率和可操控性，且更好的表示带来更大的GRPO增益；数据分配上仅需少量预训练数据。

<h3 id="587">[587] Latency-Response Theory Model: Evaluating Large Language Models via Response Accuracy and Chain-of-Thought Length</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.07019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07019) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhiyu Xu, Jia Liu, Yixin Wang, Yuqi Gu
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI; stat.AP; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Item Response Theory, Chain-of-Thought
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Toby-X/Latency-Response-Theory-Model)
- **Summary**: 提出LaTency-Response Theory (LaRT)模型，联合建模响应正确性和思维链长度，含相关参数。通过SA-EM估计并证明可识别性。在真实LLM数据上，LaRT优于IRT，提升预测力、项目效率、排名有效性和评估效率。

<h3 id="588">[588] SGM: Safety Glasses for Multimodal Large Language Models via Neuron-Level Detoxification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.15052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15052) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongbo Wang, AprilPyone MaungMaung, Isao Echizen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Vision-Language Model, Safety Alignment
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大模型的有毒输出和对抗触发，提出神经元级解毒方法SGM，通过加权软抑制重新校准毒性相关神经元，无需参数更新。在MM-TOXIC-QA上将有害率从45.0%降至4.5%，同时保持流畅性和推理能力。

<h3 id="589">[589] Kascade: A Practical Sparse Attention Method for Long-Context LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.16391](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16391) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dhruv Deshmukh, Saurabh Goyal, Nipun Kwatra, Ramachandran Ramjee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Sparse Attention, LLM Inference, Long Context
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文推理中注意力延迟瓶颈，提出训练无关的稀疏注意力方法Kascade，在锚点层计算Top-k索引并复用到中间层。在H100上实现最高4.1倍解码加速和2.2倍预填充加速，同时保持密集注意力精度。

<h3 id="590">[590] KV Admission: Learning What to Write for Efficient Long-Context LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.17452](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17452) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yen-Chieh Huang, Pi-Cheng Hsiu, Rui Fang, Ming-Syan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Cache Management
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EMCLab-Sinica/WG-KV)
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文推理的KV缓存增长问题，形式化了KV管理三原语（准入、选择、驱逐），提出Write-Gated KV (WG-KV)学习在缓存前预测token效用，减少内存并加速预填充和解码。

<h3 id="591">[591] Scoring, Reasoning, and Selecting the Best! Ensembling Large Language Models via a Peer-Review Process</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.23213](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23213) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhijun Chen, Zeyu Ji, Qianren Mao, Hao Wu, Jinhuan Song, Junhang Cheng, Bangjie Qin, Zhuoran Li, Jingzheng Li, Kai Sun, Zizhe Wang, Yikun Ban, Zhu Sun, Xiangyang Ji, Hailong Sun, Xiao Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM-as-a-Judge, Ensemble Learning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 提出无监督LLM集成方法LLM-PeerReview，通过LLM-as-a-Judge评分、图模型真值推理和最高分选择，在4个数据集上比Smoothie-Global提升6.9%和7.3%，并构建了集成基准套件。

<h3 id="592">[592] Entropy-Aware Token Rejection for Improving Speculative Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.23765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23765) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tiancheng Su, Meicong Zhang, Guoxiu He
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference, Token Selection
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ECNU-Text-Computing/EASD)
- **Summary**: 提出熵感知推测解码（EASD），在草稿模型和目标模型高不确定性且预测重叠时拒绝对齐的token并重采样，防止低置信错误传播。在推理基准上提升准确率，甚至超过目标模型单独性能，同时保持效率。

<h3 id="593">[593] DIP: Dynamic In-Context Planner For Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.03199](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03199) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yang Li, Han Meng, Chenan Wang, Zhenyu Bi, Xuan Wang, Haipeng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, Diffusion Language Model, LLM Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wmd3i/DIP)
- **Summary**: 针对扩散语言模型（DLM）沿用自回归式静态上下文学习导致计算开销大的问题，提出动态上下文规划器DIP，利用块级KV缓存机制，以平均验证置信度为信号在生成过程中动态排序和插入上下文示例。在数学与代码基准上，使用LLaDA模型实现了最高1.59倍和1.36倍加速，同时基本保持生成质量。

<h3 id="594">[594] EpiQAL: Benchmarking Large Language Models in Epidemiological Question Answering and Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.03471](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03471) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mingyang Wei, Dehai Min, Zewen Liu, Yuzhang Xie, Guanchen Wu, Ziyang Zhang, Carl Yang, Max S. Y. Lau, Qi He, Lu Cheng, Wei Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Benchmark, Epidemiological Reasoning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 现有医疗问答基准侧重临床知识，缺乏对流行病学推理的系统评估。构建EpiQAL基准，包含事实回忆、多步推理和结论重建三个子集，覆盖多种疾病文献。对15个开源与闭源LLM评估显示，模型在流行病学推理上表现有限，多步推理最具挑战，规模并非决定因素，CoT提示对多步推理有帮助。

<h3 id="595">[595] AdaFuse: Adaptive Ensemble Decoding with Test-Time Scaling for LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06022) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chengming Cui, Tianxin Wei, Ziyi Chen, Ruizhong Qiu, Zhichen Zeng, Zhining Liu, Xuying Ning, Duo Zhou, Jingrui He
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Ensemble Learning, LLM Inference, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CCM0111/AdaFuse)
- **Summary**: 现有LLM集成方法使用固定融合粒度，缺乏灵活性和任务适应性。提出AdaFuse自适应集成解码框架，基于不确定性动态决定是否在生成步骤进行集成，并在低置信度时通过多样性感知缩放探索候选续写。在开放域问答、算术推理和机器翻译上平均相对提升6.88%。

<h3 id="596">[596] FastSLM: Hierarchical Temporal Abstraction for Efficient Long-Form Speech Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06199](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06199) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junseok Lee, Chang-Jae Chun
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, Speech Processing, Token Compression
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Lee-junseok1025/FastSLM)
- **Summary**: 多模态大模型扩展至长语音时受输入token数量爆炸的瓶颈，且语音缺乏空间冗余，压缩困难。提出FastSLM和分层时间抽象器（HTA），跨多个时间尺度渐进蒸馏声学特征，实现每秒1.67 token的极端压缩率（减少97%）。在多种语音语言任务上取得竞争性能，同时显著降低token数和FLOPs。

<h3 id="597">[597] Do Language Models Reason Across Languages?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06644](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06644) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yan Meng, Wafaa Mohammed, Christof Monz
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cross-Lingual, Multi-Hop Reasoning, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 探究语言模型能否跨语言整合信息，构建了两跳多语言文档问答设置。发现模型对答案区间文档的语言变化更敏感，且多语言推理不遵循忠实的逐步分解，存在约18%的组合失败。提出三分支SUBQ提示方法，将准确率从10.1%提升至66.5%。

<h3 id="598">[598] Kinship Data Benchmark for Multi-hop Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.07794](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07794) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianda Sun, Dimitar Kazakov
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Hop Reasoning, Benchmark, Data Synthesis
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 多跳推理评估需要可控难度和文化差异的基准。提出KinshipQA，通过生成式流程生成符合不同亲属体系婚姻约束的大规模族谱数据，并据此构建文本推理任务。对六个LLM评估显示结果分布广泛，揭示模型间及文化背景下多跳推理的系统差异。

<h3 id="599">[599] Triggering Chain-of-Thought via Latent Feature Interventions in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.08058](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08058) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenghao He, Guangzhi Xiong, Bohan Liu, Sanchit Sinha, Aidong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Sparse Autoencoder, Chain-of-Thought
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Zhenghao-He/LatentCoT)
- **Summary**: CoT提示可提升推理但内部触发信号未被理解。利用稀疏自编码器识别与推理相关的潜在特征，通过定向干预可激发推理行为，无需显式CoT提示。实验表明干预少量特征即可达到与CoT相当的准确率，抑制实验证实了特征的因果作用。

<h3 id="600">[600] To Retrieve or To Think? Cross-Boundary Context Evolution for Multi-hop Complex Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.08747](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08747) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rubing Chen, Jian Wang, Wenjie Li, Xiao-Yong Wei, Qing Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Multi-Hop Reasoning, Context Selection
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Findings
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Anya-RB-Chen/EvoCtx)
- **Summary**: 现有上下文增强方法（如RAG）采用僵化检索策略，盲目扩展上下文导致计算浪费和性能下降。提出EvoCtx，将复杂推理建模为跨边界上下文演化，动态决定检索外部证据还是内部精炼推理轨迹。在开放域多跳QA基准上显著优于先前方法。

<h3 id="601">[601] AgenTRIM: Tool Risk Mitigation for Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.12449](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12449) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Roy Betser, Amit Giloni, Shamik Bose, Sindhu Padakandla, Chiara Picardi, Lidor Erez, Roman Vainshtein
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, LLM Security
- **Summary**: AI代理的工具权限不当带来安全风险，如过度代理和不足代理。提出AgenTRIM框架，离线重建并验证工具接口，运行时通过逐步最小权限过滤和状态感知验证来动态限制工具访问。在AgentDojo上大幅降低攻击成功率且保持任务性能，对描述性攻击具有鲁棒性。

<h3 id="602">[602] CORE-T: COherent REtrieval of Tables for Text-to-SQL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.13111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13111) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hassan Soliman, Vivek Gupta, Dan Roth, Iryna Gurevych
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, Table Retrieval, Dense Retrieval
- **Venue**: EMNLP Main 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/UKPLab/emnlp2026-core-t)
- **Summary**: Text-to-SQL中多表连接场景下，稠密检索返回大量干扰表，而连接感知方法依赖额外假设或高开销。提出CORE-T，利用LLM生成表用途元数据和预计算的兼容性缓存，以单次LLM调用选择连贯可连接表子集。在Bird、Spider等基准上，表选择F1提升最高22.7点，返回表减少40%，多表执行准确率提升最高24.4点且token开销更低。

<h3 id="603">[603] POCI-Diff: 3D-Layout Guided Diffusion for Controllable Synthetic Surveillance Data Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.14056](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14056) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andrea Rigo, Luca Stornaiuolo, Weijie Wang, Mauro Martino, Bruno Lepri, Nicu Sebe
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Synthetic Data Generation, 3D Vision, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 真实监控数据采集成本高且隐私受限，现有合成数据缺乏细粒度3D控制。提出POCI-Diff，结合混合潜在扩散和深度条件ControlNet，从显式3D边界框布局生成带注释合成场景，并支持无扭曲编辑。实验在视觉保真度和布局遵循上优于SOTA方法，消除扭曲伪影。

<h3 id="604">[604] Mechanism Shift During Post-training from Autoregressive to Masked Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.14758](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14758) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Injin Kong, Hyoungjoon Lee, Yohan Jo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Diffusion Language Model, Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Training
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 自回归模型后训练为掩码扩散模型后，其计算机制是继承还是重组尚不清楚。对比两个7B ARM-MDM家族在四个诊断任务上的高归因通路，发现任务依赖性机制迁移：前缀主导任务保留继承通路，全局约束任务则向更早层重组，且MDM的组件专门化更弱。

<h3 id="605">[605] Standardizing Longitudinal Radiology Report Evaluation via Large Language Model Annotation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.16753](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.16753) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyi Wang, Grazziela Figueredo, Ruizhe Li, Xin Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Data Annotation, Clinical NLP
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wxinyi1996/Standardizing-Longitudinal-Chest-X-ray-Report-Evaluation-via-Large-Language-Model-Annotation.git)
- **Summary**: 缺乏工具一致标注放射学报告中纵向时间变化以评估生成模型。提出基于LLM的标注流程，识别相关句子并提取疾病进展，比较五个LLM后选择Qwen2.5-32B标注95,169份MIMIC-CXR报告。该方法在纵向信息检测和疾病追踪上F1分别高出既有方案11.3%和5.3%。

<h3 id="606">[606] Beyond Factual QA: Mentorship-Oriented Question Answering over Long-Form Multilingual Content</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.17173](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17173) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Parth Bhalerao, Ruiwen Guan, Diola Dsouza, Oana Ignat
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Multilingual Learning, Multi-Agent System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AIM-SCU/MentorQA)
- **Summary**: 现有QA评估侧重事实正确性，而教育职业指导需要导师式回应，缺乏多语言长格式基准。提出MentorQA，包含约9000对QA、180小时四语言视频内容，定义清晰度、一致性和学习价值等维度。实验显示多Agent架构产生更高质量的导师回答，尤其对复杂主题和低资源语言，但LLM自动评估的一致性存在较大差异。

<h3 id="607">[607] The Grammar of Transformers: A Systematic Review of Interpretability Research on Syntactic Knowledge in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.19926](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19926) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nora Graichen, Iria de-Dios-Flores, Gemma Boleda
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Systematic Review, Interpretability, Syntactic Knowledge
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 对Transformer语言模型的句法能力缺乏系统性认识。综述337篇文章、3000多个数据点，覆盖句法现象、语言、模型和方法。结果表明模型编码非平凡的句法知识，正式句法表现强，句法-语义界面较弱，低资源语言性能低，且研究集中于英语和BERT类模型。

<h3 id="608">[608] Securing Time Integrity in Energy IoT Against Clock Drift and Y2K38 Failures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.23147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23147) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Saeid Jamshidi, Foutse Khomh, Carol Fung, Omar Abdul Wahab, Rolando Herrero
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Anomaly Detection, Graph Neural Network, Energy AI
- **Summary**: 能源IoT系统面临时钟漂移、同步操纵和Y2K38溢出等时间完整性威胁，传统异常检测假设时间戳可靠。提出STGAT，结合漂移感知时间嵌入、时间自注意力和图注意力建模设备间一致性，并通过Jacobian正则化分离正常与异常时间演化。实验达到95.7%准确率、94.0%精确率、92.0%召回率、F1 93.0%、AUC 0.97，检测延迟2.3时间步（比基线改进26%）。

<h3 id="609">[609] R3G: A Reasoning-Retrieval-Reranking Framework for Vision-Centric Answer Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00104](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00104) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhuohong Chen, Zhengxian Wu, Zirui Liao, Shenao Jiang, Hangrui Xu, Yang Chen, Chaokui Su, Xiaoyu Liu, Haoqian Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, RAG, Question Answering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/czh24/R3G)
- **Summary**: 针对视觉中心VQA中图像选择与推理整合困难的问题，提出R3G模块化推理-检索-重排框架，先生成推理计划，再通过粗检索和细粒度重排选择证据图像。在MRAG-Bench上，R3G在六个MLLM骨干和九个子场景中均提升准确率，取得最优总体表现。

<h3 id="610">[610] Toward Scalable Audio Description Quality Control: A Workflow for Evaluating Human and VLM Raters</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.01390](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01390) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lana Do, Gio Jung, Juvenal Francisco Barajas, Andrew Taylor Scott, Shasta Ihorn, Alexander Mario Blum, Vassilis Athitsos, Ilmi Yoon
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Accessibility Technology, Vision-Language Model, Human Evaluation
- **Venue**: ASSETS 2026
- **Summary**: 针对长篇幅音频描述(AD)质量难以规模化评估的问题，提出基于项目反应理论(IRT)的工作流，用六维框架评估VLM和人类评分者。结果发现顶级VLM可接近人类评分水平，但推理可靠性与可操作性不如人类，支持混合人机评估系统。

<h3 id="611">[611] Semi-supervised CAPP Transformer Learning via Pseudo-labeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.01419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01419) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dennis Gross, Helge Spieker, Arnaud Gotlieb, Emmanuel Stathatos, Panorios Benardos, George-Christopher Vosniakos
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Semi-Supervised Learning, Manufacturing AI, Self-Training
- **Summary**: 针对计算机辅助工艺规划(CAPP)数据稀缺导致模型泛化差的问题，提出半监督伪标签方法，利用在已有行为数据上训练的oracle过滤未标注零件的预测，再进行一次性重训练。在模拟真实数据分布的小规模数据集上持续获得准确率提升。

<h3 id="612">[612] Zero-shot Generalizable Graph Anomaly Detection with Mixture of Riemannian Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06859](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06859) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Zhao, Qingyun Sun, Jiayi Luo, Xingcheng Fu, Jianxin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Anomaly Detection, Mixture-of-Experts, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Venue**: IEEE International Conference on Data Mining (ICDM 2026)
- **Summary**: 针对零样本图异常检测忽略不同异常模式内在几何差异的问题，提出GAD-MoRE框架，包含多个不同曲率空间的黎曼专家网络、异常感知多曲率特征对齐模块和基于记忆的动态路由器。零样本实验表明其显著优于现有SOTA泛化GAD基线。

<h3 id="613">[613] Robustness of Vision Language Models Against Split-Image Harmful Input Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.08136](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08136) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Md Rafi Ur Rashid, MD Sadik Hossain Shanto, Vishnu Asutosh Dasu, Shagufta Mehnaz
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Jailbreak Attack, Adversarial Robustness
- **Venue**: ACM CCS 2026
- **Summary**: 发现VLM安全对齐仅针对整体图像，无法识别分布在多个图像碎片中的有害语义，据此提出分割图像视觉越狱攻击SIVA，从朴素分割演进到自适应白盒和黑盒迁移攻击，并用对抗知识蒸馏(Adv-KD)提升跨模型迁移性。在四个现代VLM和三个越狱数据集上最高比现有基线提升44%迁移成功率。

<h3 id="614">[614] Artifact Reduction in Undersampled 3D Cone-Beam CTs using a Hybrid 2D-3D CNN Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.08727](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08727) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Johannes Thalhammer, Tina Dorosti, Sebastian Peterhansl, Daniela Pfeiffer, Franz Pfeiffer, Florian Schaff
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, CT Reconstruction, Image Reconstruction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/J-3TO/2D-3DCNN_sparseview/)
- **Summary**: 针对欠采样3D锥束CT重建带来伪影并降低图像质量的问题，提出混合2D-3D CNN框架：先用2D U-Net逐切片提取特征，再用3D解码器利用跨切片上下文预测无伪影体积。结果在冠状面和矢状面层间一致性上显著提升，计算开销低。

<h3 id="615">[615] Small Updates, Big Doubts: Does Parameter-Efficient Fine-tuning Enhance Hallucination Detection ?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11166) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xu Hu, Yifan Zhang, Songtao Wei, Chen Zhao, Qiannan Li, Bingzhe Li, Feng Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, LLM Hallucination, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对参数高效微调(PEFT)如何影响大模型幻觉行为缺乏系统理解的问题，在三个开源LLM和三个事实型QA基准上使用七种无监督幻觉检测方法进行实证研究。结果表明PEFT一致增强幻觉检测能力，显著提升AUROC，且主要重塑不确定性编码方式而非注入新事实知识。

<h3 id="616">[616] MAS-on-the-Fly: In-Context Structural Adaptation of LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.13671](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13671) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guangyi Liu, Haojun Lin, Huan Zeng, Heng Wang, Quanming Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, RAG
- **Summary**: 针对LLM多智能体系统部署后缺乏结构适应性的问题，提出MASFly实现上下文结构自适应：通过检索增强的SOP实例化生成查询特定系统，并利用Watcher代理基于失败经验进行执行时重配置。在TravelPlanner等任务上取得SOTA，成功率61.7%。

<h3 id="617">[617] Reverse N-Wise Output-Oriented Testing for AI/ML and Quantum Computing Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14275](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14275) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lamine Rihani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Testing, Quantum Computing, Test Generation
- **Summary**: 针对AI/ML与量子计算软件在概率性输出、多维交互等测试挑战，提出反向n元输出测试范式，在输出等价类、校准分桶、量子测量分布等上构造覆盖数组，并通过无梯度元启发优化求解黑盒逆映射以生成触发目标行为的输入。该框架在故障检测率、测试效率等方面带来改善。

<h3 id="618">[618] ST-EVO: Towards Generative Spatio-Temporal Evolution of Multi-Agent Communication Topologies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14681) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xingjian Wu, Xvyuan Liu, Junkai Lu, Siyuan Wang, Xiangfei Qiu, Yang Shu, Jilin Hu, Chenjuan Guo, Bin Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Flow Matching, Multi-Agent Coordination
- **Summary**: 针对自进化多智能体系统仅沿单一空间或时间维度演化的问题，提出ST-EVO，使用基于流匹配的调度器实现对话级通信调度，并具备不确定性感知和自反馈能力。在九个基准上取得SOTA，准确率提升约5%-25%。

<h3 id="619">[619] DesignAsCode: Bridging Structural Editability and Visual Fidelity in Graphic Design Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.17690](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17690) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziyuan Liu, Shizhao Sun, Danqing Huang, Yingdong Shi, Meisheng Zhang, Ji Li, Jingsong Yu, Jiang Bian
- **Subjects**: cs.GR; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Layout Generation, Code Generation, Image Generation
- **Summary**: 针对图形设计生成中视觉保真度与结构可编辑性难以兼顾的问题，提出DesignAsCode，将设计视为HTML/CSS程序化合成，采用计划-实现-反思流水线，包含语义规划器和视觉感知反思机制。实验显示其在结构有效性和美学质量上显著超过基线，并支持布局重定向、文档生成和CSS动画。

<h3 id="620">[620] Social-JEPA: Emergent Geometric Isomorphism</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02263](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02263) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoran Zhang, Youjin Wang, Yi Duan, Rong Fu, Dianyu Zhao, Sicheng Fan, Shuaishuai Cao, Wentao Guo, Xiao Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Representation Learning, World Model
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体从不同视角学习世界模型能否产生共享表征的问题，让独立智能体在无参数共享下以JEPA方式训练，发现其潜空间出现近似线性等距关系。利用该对齐可将一个智能体训练的分类器零梯度迁移到另一个，并加速后续学习、降低计算量（注：论文因结果争议已撤稿）。

<h3 id="621">[621] Toward Generalizable Deep Learning Based Peatland Fire Detection via Walsh Hadamard Transform and Domain Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02465) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Emadeldeen Hamdan, Ahmad Faiz Tharima, Mohd Zahirasri Mohd Tohir, Dayang Nur Sakinah Musa, Erdem Koyuncu, Adam J. Watts, Ahmet Enis Cetin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wildfire Detection, Domain Adaptation, Transfer Learning
- **Venue**: Signal, Image and Video Processing (SVIP)
- **Summary**: 针对常规野火检测器难以识别泥炭地火灾（阴燃、低火焰强度等）的问题，提出WHT-ResNet-50框架，利用Walsh-Hadamard变换增强特征并降低复杂度，并通过结构重参数化降低推理成本，结合迁移学习和混合域训练实现野火-泥炭地统一检测。实验显示准确率和F1更高、参数更少，视频测试事件检出率达100%。

<h3 id="622">[622] Phys4D: Fine-Grained Physics-Consistent 4D Modeling from Video Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03485](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03485) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haoran Lu, Shang Wu, Songling Liu, Jianshu Zhang, Maojiang Su, Guo Ye, Chenwei Xu, Lie Lu, Pranav Maneriker, Fan Du, Zhaoran Wang, Han Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Video Generation, 4D Reconstruction, Physics-Informed Learning
- **Summary**: 针对视频扩散模型缺乏细粒度物理一致性的问题，提出Phys4D三阶段训练流程：伪监督预训练引导几何运动，模拟数据物理监督微调，再经模拟接地强化学习修正残留物理违例。实验表明其在时空与物理一致性上显著优于外观驱动基线，同时保持生成性能。

<h3 id="623">[623] MIND: Unified Inquiry and Diagnosis RL with Criteria Grounded Clinical Supports for Psychiatric Consultation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03677](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03677) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guoyi Li, Shihao Xu, Jiatong Ma, Zhongjiang Yao, Yunyun Han, Jianhua Chen, Yafeng Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Dialogue System, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Lingxi-mental-health/MIND.Å)
- **Summary**: 针对精神科咨询中证据、诊断标准与支持信息纠缠不清的问题，提出MIND标准接地证据状态决策接口，构建包含已观测证据、未解决检查、鉴别诊断及支持可靠性元数据的类型化状态，并由精神病学推理库提供门控支持。在EMR模拟协议下比强基线准确率提升8.8和7.3个点，并能迁移到公共MDD-5k对话。

<h3 id="624">[624] Scale-Plan: Scalable Language-Enabled Task Planning for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.08814](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08814) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Piyush Gupta, Sangjae Bae, Jiachen Li, David Isele
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Task Planning, LLM Agent, Multi-Robot System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/honda-research-institute/Scale_Plan)
- **Summary**: 针对异构多机器人长时任务规划中感知信息量大、LLM易幻觉且接地弱的问题，提出Scale-Plan，通过PDDL域规范构建动作图，用浅层LLM推理引导结构图搜索以筛选最小相关动作和对象子集，实现高效分解与规划。在AI2-THOR基准上超过纯LLM和混合LLM-PDDL基线，提升可扩展性与可靠性。

<h3 id="625">[625] Personalized Group Relative Policy Optimization for Heterogenous Preference Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.10009](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10009) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jialu Wang, Heinrich Peters, Asad A. Butt, Navid Hashemi, Alireza Hashemi, Pouya M. Ghari, Joseph Hoover, James Rae, Morteza Dehghani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Preference Optimization, LLM Personalization
- **Summary**: 标准RLHF/GRPO优化单一全局目标，忽视用户异质性偏好；GRPO的组归一化假设样本可交换，混淆不同用户奖励分布，偏向主流偏好。提出P-GRPO，将优势估计与即时批次统计解耦，按偏好组历史奖励归一化优势，保留对比信号。实验表明P-GRPO比标准GRPO收敛更快、奖励更高，能恢复异质性偏好且不牺牲泛化能力。

<h3 id="626">[626] Risk-Adjusted Harm Scoring for Automated Red Teaming for LLMs in Financial Services</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.10807](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10807) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fabrizio Dimino, Bhaskarjit Sarmah, Stefano Pasquali
- **Subjects**: q-fin.CP; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Red Teaming, LLM Safety, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 现有LLM安全评估依赖二元攻击成功率与领域无关分类，无法覆盖金融等受监管场景中通过合法/专业框架触发的失败。提出RAHS风险调整伤害评分（综合披露严重性、免责缓解、法官一致性），构建FinRedTeamBench基准（989提示，7个风险领域），并用多LLM法官集成与自适应多轮红队流水线评估9个开源模型。RAHS在接近满分的ASR下仍保持区分度，排名对超参数稳定；多轮压力暴露更严重的运营泄露，发现单轮评估无法揭示的失败模式。

<h3 id="627">[627] Efficient Cross-View Localization in 6G Space-Air-Ground Integrated Network</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.11398](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11398) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Min Hao, Yanbing Xu, Maoqiang Wu, Jinglin Huang, Chen Shang, Jiacheng Wang, Jiawen Kang, Zhu Han, Wei Ni
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cross-View Localization, 6G, Distributed Inference
- **Summary**: 视觉定位的跨视角方法可增强覆盖与适应性，但面临延迟、能耗和隐私问题；6G SAGIN架构可提供支持。提出将跨视角定位（CVL）与6G SAGIN集成，设计分割推理框架，联合优化通信、计算与机密性。实验验证框架有效性，并提供优化问题解决方案。

<h3 id="628">[628] Grammar of the Wave: Towards Explainable Multivariate Time Series Event Detection via Neuro-Symbolic VLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.11479](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11479) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sky Chenwei Wan, Yifei Y. Wang, Tianjun Hou, Xiqing Chang, Aymeric Jan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Time Series Event Detection, Neuro-Symbolic AI, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 时间序列事件检测（TSED）需将文本描述对应到多变量信号区间，但密集标注昂贵，监督学习困难。提出事件逻辑树（ELT）表示将语言描述转为结构化时序逻辑；SELA神经符号VLM代理框架迭代从信号可视化中接地原语并按ELT约束组合；发布跨能源和气候基准。SELA超越监督微调和现有零/少样本时间序列推理基线。

<h3 id="629">[629] EReCu: Pseudo-label Evolution Fusion and Refinement with Multi-Cue Learning for Unsupervised Camouflage Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.11521](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11521) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuo Jiang, Gaojia Zhang, Min Tan, Yufei Yin, Gang Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Camouflaged Object Detection, Unsupervised Learning, Object Detection
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/JSLiam94/EReCu)
- **Summary**: 无监督伪装目标检测（UCOD）因目标与背景高相似及噪声伪标签而困难；现有细化策略忽略内在感知线索，导致边界溢出和结构模糊。提出统一UCOD框架：多线索本征感知模块整合低层纹理与中层语义；伪标签进化融合通过师生交互和深度可分离卷积去噪，光谱张量注意力融合平衡语义与结构；局部伪标签细化利用注意力多样性恢复细节。在多个UCOD数据集上达SOTA，具有更优细节感知、边界对齐和强泛化。

<h3 id="630">[630] The Landscape of Generative AI in Information Systems: A Synthesis of Secondary Reviews and Research Agendas</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.11842](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11842) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aleksander Jarzębowicz, Adam Przybyłek, Jacinto Estima, Yen Ying Ng, Jakub Swacha, Beata Zielosko, Lech Madeyski, Noel Carroll, Kai-Kristian Kemell, Bartosz Marcinkowski, Alberto Rodrigues da Silva, Viktoria Stray, Netta Iivari, Anh Nguyen-Duc, Jorge Melegati, Boris Delibašić, Emilio Insfran
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Systematic Review, AI Governance, Human-AI Collaboration
- **Summary**: GenAI在信息系统（IS）中的收益与挑战分散，缺乏综合认知。系统综述28篇二次研究/路线图论文，采用文献计量与主题分析，并进行质量评估。GenAI有变革潜力但受技术不可靠、社会伦理风险、治理真空制约；提出研究议程，强调混合人机协同、情境验证、概率系统设计原则和适应性治理。

<h3 id="631">[631] An Empirical Investigation of Pre-Trained Deep Learning Model Reuse in the Scientific Process</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.13584](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13584) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nicholas M. Synovic, Karolina Ryzka, Alessandra V. Vellucci Solari, Kenny Lyons, James C. Davis, George K. Thiruvathukal
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transfer Learning, Empirical Analysis, Open Science
- **Venue**: 22nd IEEE International Conference on eScience
- **Summary**: 从零训练深度学习模型成本高昂，自然科学家复用预训练模型（PTM）的模式缺乏实证研究。对17,718篇开放获取论文进行实证研究，量化PTM在自然科学领域的复用模式。生化/遗传/分子生物学领先；适配复用最常见；测试阶段受PTM集成影响最大。

<h3 id="632">[632] PA3: Policy-Aware Agent Alignment through Chain-of-Thought</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.14602](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14602) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shubhashis Roy Dipta, Daniel Bis, Kun Zhou, Lichao Wang, Benjamin Z. Yao, Chenlei Guo, Ruhi Sarikaya
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Chain-of-Thought, Instruction Following
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: LLM对话助手遵循复杂业务规则困难；在上下文中包含所有策略导致高延迟和长上下文性能下降。提出多阶段对齐方法，教模型在推理时通过链式思维回忆和应用相关业务策略，无需完整策略上下文；引入基于Jaccard分数的Policy Recall奖励和幻觉惩罚用于GRPO训练。最佳模型比基线高16分，比同类模型的内上下文基线高3分，且少用40%词。

<h3 id="633">[633] Interpretable Predictability-Based AI Text Detection: A Replication Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.15034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15034) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Adam Skurla, Dominik Macko, Jakub Simko
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Text Detection, Stylometry, Research Reproducibility
- **Summary**: 复制AuTexTification人机文本归属任务；因数据划分和模型可用性差异无法精确复制。测试更新多语言模型并添加26个文档级风格特征，使用消融、置换重要性和SHAP分析；对英语和西语共享配置。共享多语言配置性能与语言特定基线相当或更好，在归属子任务提升明显；风格特征贡献小且在种子方差内。

<h3 id="634">[634] Do Large Language Models Possess a Theory of Mind? A Comparative Evaluation Using the Strange Stories Paradigm</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.18007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18007) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anna Babarczy, Andras Lukacs, Peter Vedres, Zeteny Bujka
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Theory of Mind, LLM Evaluation, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 探究LLM是否具备心理理论（ToM）能力，其输出是真实理解还是模式补全。用改编的Strange Stories文本测试，比较5个LLM与人类对照。模型间有差距；较旧较小的模型受推理线索数量和干扰信息影响大；GPT-4o在最具挑战条件下与人类相当。

<h3 id="635">[635] PhyGile: Physics-Prefix Guided Motion Generation for Agile General Humanoid Motion Tracking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.19305](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19305) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiacheng Bao, Haoran Yang, Yucheng Xin, Junhong Liu, Yuecheng Xu, Han Liang, Pengfei Han, Xiaoguang Ma, Dong Wang, Bin Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Motion Generation, Humanoid Robot, Physics-Informed Learning
- **Summary**: 文本到动作生成模型基于人类数据训练，直接重定向到人形机器人常违反物理可行性。提出PhyGile框架，在推理时用物理引导前缀直接生成机器人原生动作（262维骨骼空间），课程式混合专家训练GMT控制器，后训练提升鲁棒性，物理前缀适配微调。扩展了文本驱动人形控制的边界，在真实机器人上稳定跟踪敏捷高难动作。

<h3 id="636">[636] Mixture-Greedy for Online Generative Model Selection: Is UCB Necessary in Diversity-Aware Multi-Armed Bandits?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.21716](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21716) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bahar Dibaei Nia, Farzan Farnia
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Multi-Armed Bandit, Generative Model, Exploration
- **Summary**: 多样性感知的多臂老虎机（MAB）中生成模型选择，UCB探索奖励可能降低采样效率。提出简单Mixture-Greedy策略，无显式UCB乐观；给出理论解释：多样性目标诱导内隐探索。Mixture-Greedy收敛更快且性能更优（FID和Vendi），并具有亚线性遗憾保证。

<h3 id="637">[637] T-MAP: Red-Teaming LLM Agents with Trajectory-aware Evolutionary Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.22341](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22341) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hyomin Lee, Sangwoo Park, Yumin Choi, Sohyun An, Seanie Lee, Sung Ju Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Red Teaming, LLM Security
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/pwnhyo/T-MAP)
- **Summary**: 现有红队主要诱导有害文本，忽略LLM代理在多步工具执行中的漏洞，尤其MCP生态。提出轨迹感知进化搜索T-MAP，利用执行轨迹引导对抗提示发现，可自动生成绕过安全护栏并通过工具交互实现有害目标的攻击。在多种MCP环境中ARR显著优于基线，对GPT-5.2等前沿模型有效。

<h3 id="638">[638] Variable-Length Audio Fingerprinting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.23947](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23947) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongjie Chen, Hanyu Meng, Huimin Zeng, Ryan A. Rossi, Lie Lu, Josh Kimball
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Audio Fingerprinting, Audio Retrieval, Audio Processing
- **Venue**: ACM MM 2026
- **Summary**: 现有深度音频指纹方法固定长度分段，忽略时间动态。提出VLAFP，首个支持变长音频指纹的深度模型，训练和测试均可处理变长。在三个真实数据集上，实时音频识别和检索超越SOTA。

<h3 id="639">[639] M2K: Making the Model-Kernel Interface Explicit for Reliable CUDA Kernel Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.24595](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24595) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mengting He, Shihao Xia, Haomin Jia, Wenfei Wu, Linhai Song
- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Symbolic Execution, Program Verification, GPU Computing, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: LLM推理系统依赖CUDA内核，模型-内核接口隐式且不明，导致内存错误。提出M2K框架：HFProbe无GPU追踪模型执行并分类内核参数，cuKLEE符号执行CUDA内核，将张量建模为独立内存区域，符号化线程ID。在真实LLM推理系统中发现181个未知错误，仅9个误报。

<h3 id="640">[640] Beyond Static Visual Tokens: Structured Sequential Visual Chain-of-Thought Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.26737](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26737) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Guangfu Guo, Xiaoqian Lu, Yue Feng, Mingming Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 多模态LLM将图像编码为静态视觉前缀，缺乏目标驱动和自适应视觉访问。提出结构化顺序视觉CoT（SSV-CoT）：问题相关显著性图识别关键区域，按判别顺序推理；端到端训练，无需区域标注或外部工具。在多个视觉推理基准上取得提升。

<h3 id="641">[641] Can We Change the Stroke Size for Easier Diffusion?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.26783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26783) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yunwei Bai, Ying Kiat Tan, Yao Shu, Tsuhan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 本文针对扩散模型在低信噪比下的像素级预测难题，类比油画用最细笔画，提出笔画大小控制（stroke-size control）这一受控干预手段，通过调整跨时间步的监督目标、预测和扰动的粗糙度来简化预测目标。目前为概念性研究，未报告量化指标。

<h3 id="642">[642] Robust Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Small UAS Separation Assurance under GPS Degradation and Spoofing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.28900](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28900) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alex Zongo, Filippos Fotiadis, Ufuk Topcu, Peng Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, UAV, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 该论文针对小型无人机系统在GPS降级与欺骗下的安全间隔保持问题，提出将状态观测扰动建模为智能体与对手的零和博弈，并推导出对抗扰动的闭式表达式，从而线性时间计算扰动并集成到多智能体强化学习策略中。在高密度sUAS仿真中，该鲁棒策略在高达35%的扰动概率下实现近零碰撞率，优于未含对抗扰动训练的基线。

<h3 id="643">[643] Where Does Robustness Live? Neuron-Guided Adaptation for Retrieval-Augmented Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.02194](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02194) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jae O Lee, Jaemin Kim, Sumyeong Ahn, Seo Yeon Park
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Instruction Tuning, LLM Robustness
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HYU-ARK-Lab/NeuRIT)
- **Summary**: 本文针对检索增强语言模型在面对噪声或无关上下文时脆弱的问题，指出需要弃权与选择性提取两种能力，但现有方法未分开训练且适配粒度粗糙。提出NeuRIT，一种基于神经元定位的鲁棒指令微调框架，挖掘上下文相关神经元作为锚点进行选择性适配，并通过两阶段指令微调分别学习抑制生成与提取证据。在多样QA基准和生成器主干上持续优于强基线。

<h3 id="644">[644] Interactive Clarification for Cloud Infrastructure-as-Code Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.02382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02382) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenning Yang, Kaden Gruizenga, Tongyuan Miao, Patrick Tser Jern Kon, Hui Guan, Andrew Barto, Ang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Infrastructure-as-Code, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 本文针对LLM生成基础设施即代码时用户请求模糊且测试昂贵的问题，提出免训练的多层消歧框架：生成多样候选规格、识别结构分歧、按信息量排序并产生针对性澄清问题，以逐步缩小配置空间。同时引入专家验证的Ambig-IaC基准（300个任务）。实验表明该方法优于现有交互澄清基线，增益随交互预算增加且跨模型泛化。

<h3 id="645">[645] Unveiling Language Routing Isolation in Multilingual MoE Models for Interpretable Subnetwork Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.03592](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03592) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kening Zheng, Wei-Chieh Huang, Jiahao Huo, Zhonghao Li, Henry Peng Zou, Yibo Yan, Xin Zou, Jungang Li, Junzhuo Li, Hanrong Zhang, Xuming Hu, Philip S. Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Multilingual Learning
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对多语言MoE模型在不同语言上性能差异大且内部机制不明的问题，系统分析专家路由模式，揭示语言路由隔离现象，并提出RISE框架，利用路由隔离识别并适配语言特定专家子网，采用三部分选择策略（特异性分数和重叠分数）。在10种语言上目标语言F1提升最高10.85%，且跨语言退化极小。

<h3 id="646">[646] Do We Still Need Humans in the Loop? Human vs. LLM Annotation in Active Learning for TikTok Hate Speech Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.13899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13899) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ahmad Dawar Hakimi, Lea Hirlimann, Isabelle Augenstein, Hinrich Schütze
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Active Learning, Data Annotation, Hate Speech Detection
- **Summary**: 本文针对标注成本高的问题，在28万条德国政治TikTok评论数据集上比较了主动学习中人工与LLM标注的效果。结果发现LLM标注在约十分之一成本下优于人工监督分类器，但优势依赖于特定的两问题分解标注接口；主动学习在预过滤池中无可靠优势，人类仍作为评估者不可或缺。

<h3 id="647">[647] Perturbation Sensitivity of Maximum-Likelihood Pairwise Ranking in Computational Decision Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17805) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junyi Yao, Zihao Zheng, Jiayu Long
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.GT
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Robustness Evaluation
- **Venue**: 2026 International Conference on Data Science, Mathematics, and Informatics (ICoDMI)
- **Summary**: 本文针对最大似然成对排序在比较数据结构化扰动下的敏感性刻画不足问题，将协调扰动形式化为预算受限子集选择问题，并引入自适应子集选择攻击（ASSA）作为可扩展搜索启发式。在合成和真实偏好数据上显示排序具有显著状态依赖敏感性，小规模协调扰动即可诱导有意义的排序变化。

<h3 id="648">[648] KoALa-Bench: Evaluating Large Audio Language Models on Korean Speech Understanding and Faithfulness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.19782](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19782) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jinyoung Kim, Hyeongsoo Lim, Eunseo Seo, Minho Jang, Keunwoo Choi, Seungyoun Shin, Ji Won Yoon
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, Benchmark, Multilingual Learning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对大型音频语言模型在非英语语言（尤其是韩语）上基准稀缺的问题，提出KoALa-Bench，包含六项任务：四项评估语音理解（ASR、语音翻译、语音问答、语音指令跟随），两项评估语音忠实度，并纳入韩国高考听力及文化领域内容。对六种白盒和黑盒模型进行了大量实验，基准和排行榜已公开。

<h3 id="649">[649] Breaking MCP with Function Hijacking Attacks: Novel Threats for Function Calling and Agentic Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.20994](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20994) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yannis Belkhiter, Giulio Zizzo, Sergio Maffeis, Seshu Tirupathi, John D. Kelleher
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Tool Learning, Prompt Injection
- **Venue**: TMLR
- **Summary**: 本文针对函数调用LLM面临的新安全漏洞，提出函数劫持攻击（FHA），操纵智能体模型的工具选择过程以强制调用攻击者选择的函数，且与上下文语义无关。在Berkeley函数调用排行榜上，四个LLM的保留查询ASR达62.5%-81.9%，跨模型迁移ASR为11.2%-27.6%，表明需要更强的智能体系统防护措施。

<h3 id="650">[650] SketchVLM: Vision language models can annotate images to explain thoughts and guide users</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.22875](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22875) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Brandon Collins, Logan Bolton, Hung Huy Nguyen, Mohammad Reza Taesiri, Trung Bui, Anh Totti Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, SVG Generation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对VLM仅以文本作答导致用户难以验证的问题，提出SketchVLM，一个训练免费、模型无关的框架，让VLM生成非破坏性、可编辑的SVG覆盖层来可视化解释答案。在七个基准上，该方法将视觉推理准确率提升最多28.5个百分点，标注质量提升至1.48倍，且注解更忠实于模型陈述的答案。

<h3 id="651">[651] When Chain-of-Thought Fails, the Solution Hides in the Hidden States</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23351](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23351) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Houman Mehrafarin, Amit Parekh, Ioannis Konstas
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对链式思维（CoT）中间推理是否包含任务相关信息的问题，在GSM8K上进行机械因果分析，通过激活修补将CoT生成的token级隐藏状态迁移到直接回答运行。结果显示修补后的生成准确率显著高于直接提示和原始CoT，任务相关信息在正确CoT中更丰富且集中在中后层，修补输出更短但更准确，表明完整推理链并非总是必要。

<h3 id="652">[652] Inverting Foundation Models of Brain Function with Simulation-Based Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23865](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23865) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Niels Leif Bracher, Xavier Intes, Stefan T. Radev
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Simulation-Based Inference, Brain Foundation Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on Structured Probabilistic Inference & Generative Modeling
- **Summary**: 本文研究了是否可以从脑功能基础模型生成的合成脑活动中逆向恢复刺激属性，采用TRIBEv2脑仿真器与LLM生成新闻标题，使用基于仿真的推断学习从脑图到潜在刺激参数的概率映射。结果表明这些参数可从预测脑图中恢复，证明仿真神经编码保留了受控刺激维度的信息，为脑基础模型的逆向设计和解码迈出一步。

<h3 id="653">[653] CF-VLA: Efficient Coarse-to-Fine Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.24622](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24622) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fan Du, Feng Yan, Jianxiong Wu, Xinrun Xu, Weiye Zhang, Weinong Wang, Yu Guo, Bin Qian, Zhihai He, Fei Wang, Heng Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Flow Matching, Robot Manipulation
- **Venue**: ACM Multimedia (ACM MM) 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/EmbodiedAI-RoboTron/CF-VLA)
- **Summary**: 本文针对基于流的VLA策略因多步推断从高斯噪声恢复动作结构而效率低下的问题，提出CF-VLA，粗到精两阶段生成：粗阶段学习端点速度的条件后验构建动作感知起点，精阶段单步局部修正残差。实验表明在低NFE下显著优于现有NFE=2方法，匹配或超越NFE=10基线，延迟降低75.4%，真实机器人成功率83.0%。

<h3 id="654">[654] Star-Fusion: A Multi-modal Transformer Architecture for Discrete Celestial Orientation via Spherical Topology</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.26582](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26582) (replaced)
- **Authors**: May Hammad, Menatallh Hammad
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Spacecraft, Satellite Control, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 本文针对传统Lost-in-Space算法计算开销大且对噪声敏感的问题，提出Star-Fusion，将姿态估计重构为离散拓扑分类任务，采用球面K-Means聚类解决坐标缠绕，并使用SwinV2、卷积热图与坐标MLP三部分融合结构。在合成Hipparcos数据集上Top-1准确率93.4%，Top-3 97.8%，推理延迟18.4ms，适合资源受限星上部署。

<h3 id="655">[655] Secret Stealing Attacks on Local LLM Fine-Tuning through Supply-Chain Model Code Backdoors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.27426](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.27426) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zi Li, Tian Zhou, Wenze Li, Jingyu Hua, Yunlong Mao, Sheng Zhong
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Backdoor Attack, Data Poisoning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对本地微调数据中敏感秘密泄露的问题，指出被动预训练权重投毒对稀疏高熵目标无效，提出利用伪装成标准架构定义的恶意模型代码实施主动执行劫持，并设计确定性全链记忆机制、值梯度解耦注入和黑盒可验证盗取。在默认LoRA设置下Strict ASR超过98%，且能规避语义过滤、代码审计和困惑度检测。

<h3 id="656">[656] AirFM-DDA: Air-Interface Foundation Model in the Delay-Doppler-Angle Domain for AI-Native 6G</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00020) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kejia Bian, Meixia Tao, Jianhua Mo, Zhiyong Chen, Leyan Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Wireless Foundation Model, 6G Networks, Channel Estimation
- **Summary**: 本文针对现有无线基础模型在空时频域处理CSI导致多径分量纠缠且全局注意力开销大的问题，提出AirFM-DDA，在延迟-多普勒-角度域重新参数化CSI以分离多径，采用窗口注意力与帧结构感知位置编码。零样本迁移至未见城市时NMSE较最强基线提升4.9-8.5 dB，仅用10%标注数据波束预测Top-1提高12.0个百分点、LoS识别F1提高3.4个百分点，训练和推断成本降低近一个数量级。

<h3 id="657">[657] Concepts Whisper: Spectral Anti-Concentration and the Dual Geometry of Transformer Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01609](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01609) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pratyush Acharya, Nuraj Rimal, Habish Dhakal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Geometry, Mechanistic Interpretability, Activation Probing
- **Venue**: EMNLP Main Conference
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了Transformer概念表示在谱空间中的反集中现象，并探讨因果内积方法是否有助于跨语言概念迁移。通过多种提取方法分析，发现概念表示在低方差方向上系统性地反集中，而反嵌入行对比则集中在高方差方向。在17个模型上的实验证实了这一双重几何，且未发现白化因果对齐优于谱正则化的证据。

<h3 id="658">[658] Asymmetric On-Policy Distillation: Bridging Exploitation and Imitation at the Token Level</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.06387](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06387) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nan Jia, Haojin Yang, Xing Ma, Jiesong Lian, Shuailiang Zhang, Weipeng Zhang, Ke Zeng, Xunliang Cai, Zequn Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 标准在线蒸馏存在高方差、零优势区梯度消失和探索瓶颈等结构性问题。论文提出非对称在线蒸馏（AOPD），用局部散度最小化替代非正优势区域的无效负强化，同时保留正强化学习。在数学推理基准上，AOPD平均提升4.09/8.34，并保持更高的策略熵和更好的能力保持。

<h3 id="659">[659] DRIP-R: A Benchmark for Decision-Making and Reasoning Under Real-World Policy Ambiguity in the Retail Domain</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.07699](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07699) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hsuvas Borkakoty, Sebastian Pohl, Cheng Wang, Bei Chen, Yufang Hou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Venue**: EMNLP Findings
- **Summary**: 现有智能体基准通常假设策略明确，忽略了现实世界中普遍存在的策略歧义。论文提出DRIP-R基准，系统利用零售领域真实策略歧义构建场景，包含人物角色、全双工对话模拟和多方裁判评估。实验表明前沿模型在相同歧义场景上根本性分歧，证实歧义对LLM决策是系统性挑战。

<h3 id="660">[660] Robust Multi-Agent LLMs under Byzantine Faults</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09076](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09076) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haejoon Lee, Vincent-Daniel Yun, Dimitra Panagou, Sai Praneeth Karimireddy
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Byzantine Resilience
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main
- **Summary**: LLM智能体在P2P网络协作时易受拜占庭故障影响，传播错误信息。论文提出自锚共识（SAC），一种去中心化的过滤-精炼协议，通过迭代交换、评估和精炼来抑制不可靠信息。实验证明SAC在多种通信拓扑下有效抑制拜占庭影响，持续提升性能，而现有方法在攻击下显著退化。

<h3 id="661">[661] Drop the Act: Probe-Filtered RL for Faithful Chain-of-Thought Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11467) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Swapnil Parekh, Naman Goyal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Chain-of-Thought, Reinforcement Learning, Faithfulness
- **Summary**: 推理模型常产生"推理表演"，即事后合理化的步骤不贡献于正确性。论文提出ProFIL，在冻结基座模型上训练探针检测提交后步骤，并在GRPO中将相应优势置零。在四个推理领域，ProFIL减少11-100%的推理表演，提升忠实占比最多+24pp，缩短链长4-19%，同时保持或提升准确率。

<h3 id="662">[662] Large Language Models for Agentic NetOps and AIOps: Architectures, Evaluation, and Safety</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.12729](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12729) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Bilal, Jon Crowcroft, Ruizhi Wang, Xiaolong Xu, Schahram Dustdar
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Network Management, Survey
- **Summary**: LLM在网络运维（NetOps/AIOps）中应用日益广泛，但能否获得与授权相匹配的运行保障是核心问题。该综述引入运行保障契约，将自主级别与工具、证据、门控、预算等绑定，并分析了能力-保障差距。结果表明读导向和工具诊断证据较强，而配置变更和闭环操作证据不足，评价应转向工作流级。

<h3 id="663">[663] IntentVLA: Short-Horizon Intent Modeling for Aliased Robot Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14712](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14712) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shijie Lian, Bin Yu, Xiaopeng Lin, Zhaolong Shen, Laurence Tianruo Yang, Yurun Jin, Haishan Liu, Changti Wu, Hang Yuan, Cong Huang, Kai Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Vision-Language Model, Embodied AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ZGC-EmbodyAI/IntentVLA)
- **Summary**: 机器人模仿数据具有多模态性，帧条件VLA策略在部分可观测下会重采样意图，导致块间冲突。论文提出IntentVLA，编码近期视觉观察为紧凑短视距意图表示，并引入AliasBench基准。在AliasBench、SimplerEnv、LIBERO和RoboCasa上，IntentVLA提升了rollout稳定性并超越强VLA基线。

<h3 id="664">[664] Prefix-Adaptive Block Diffusion for Efficient Document Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.16861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16861) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mingxu Chai, Ziyu Shen, Chenyu Liu, Jihua Kang, Tao Gui, Qi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Document Understanding, Parallel Decoding
- **Summary**: 块扩散模型将去噪和缓存绑定在固定块边界，导致并行度受限和缓存延迟。论文提出前缀自适应块扩散模型（PA-BDM），用因果去噪和动态前缀提交恢复并行解码空间。实验显示3B PA-BDM识别分数更高，推理吞吐量比2.5B MinerU-Diffusion提升71.6%。

<h3 id="665">[665] Adversarial Trust Poisoning in Vehicular Collaborative Perception</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22122](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22122) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yutong Liu, Chenyi Wang, Ming F. Li, Qingzhao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Collaborative Perception, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 协作感知中的一致性防御本身可能被利用来毒化良性车辆的信任。论文提出TrustFlip攻击，通过物理对抗物体引发良性车辆间不一致，使防御机制误判目标车辆。结果表明攻击在最多87.7%场景中移除目标车辆，AP下降最多13%；提出的TrustReflect可降低35-100%攻击成功率。

<h3 id="666">[666] Making the Discrete Continuous: Synthetic RAW Augmentations for Fine-Grained Evaluation of Person Detection Performance in Low Light</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22455](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22455) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Valeria Pais, Malena Mendilaharzu, Daniele Faccio, Luis Oala, Christoph Clausen, Bruno Sanguinetti
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Data Augmentation, Low-Light Enhancement, Object Detection
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 AUTOPILOT Workshop
- **Summary**: 真实数据集稀疏且分布不均，难以评估低光行人检测性能。论文使用合成RAW图像增强技术生成匹配传感器噪声模型的低光样本，用于连续采样输入空间。实验显示真实与合成低光数据上的性能指标相似，表明模型难以区分两者。

<h3 id="667">[667] Measuring the Depth of LLM Unlearning via Activation Patching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24614](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24614) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jaeung Lee, Dohyun Kim, Jaemin Jo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/gnueaj/unlearning-depth-score)
- **Summary**: 现有输出级指标无法检测未学习模型中残留知识。论文提出未学习深度分数（UDS），通过激活修补量化目标知识的机械深度。在150个未学习模型上的元评估中，UDS忠实性和鲁棒性最高，并能发现被表示偏移掩盖的残留知识。

<h3 id="668">[668] When the Strongest Teacher Is Not the Best Teacher: Student-Centric Answer Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.26872](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26872) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhengyu Hu, Zheyuan Xiao, Linxin Song, Fengqing Jiang, Yuetai Li, Zhihan Xiong, Yue Liu, Junhao Lin, Yao Su, Lijie Hu, Kaize Ding, Teng Xiao, Radha Poovendran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Data Selection, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 最强教师的答案不一定是最适合特定学生的监督。论文提出以学生为中心的答案采样（SCAS），基于token级梯度分解推导高效前向代理来评估学习成本。在30个教师模型、6个基座模型和6个任务上，SCAS一致提升学生性能，表明应匹配学生而非教师强度。

<h3 id="669">[669] QUACK: Questioning, Understanding, and Auditing Communicated Knowledge in Multimodal Social Deduction Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27068](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27068) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ye Yuan, Rui Song, Weien Li, Zeyu Li, Haochen Liu, Xiangyu Kong, Changjiang Han, Yonghan Yang, Zichen Zhao, Zixuan Dong, Fuyuan Lyu, Bowei He, Haolun Wu, Jikun Kang, Xue Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Social Deduction Game, LLM Hallucination
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/AAAAA-Academia-Attractions/QUACK)
- **Summary**: 社交推理游戏环境通常仅以结果评分且多为文本交互，难以审计语言是否基于感知和行动。论文提出QUACK环境与评估框架，通过语句验证流水线重建轨迹并检查声明，标记空间幻觉、无根据指控等。结果显示最强智能体有15.1%的空间声明产生幻觉，11.5%的指控无根据。

<h3 id="670">[670] BIRDS: Characterizing and Understanding Biodiversity Impact of Large Language Model Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27480](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27480) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianyao Shi, Yi Ding
- **Subjects**: q-bio.OT; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: AI Sustainability, LLM Serving, Ecological AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TianyaoShi/BIRDS)
- **Summary**: LLM服务对生物多样性的环境影响尚未被量化。论文提出BIRDS框架，定义请求级功能单元，量化运营和体现的生物多样性影响，并提出质量归一化生物多样性影响（QNBI）。实验揭示生物多样性影响随规模累积，并暴露质量感知的服务权衡。

<h3 id="671">[671] Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning for LLM Depth Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27786](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27786) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Vincent-Daniel Yun, Youngrae Kim, Woosang Lim, YoungJin Heo, Minkyu Kim, Sunwoo Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Pruning, LLM Inference
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/daniel-eai/LoRP-Locality-Aware-Redundancy-Pruning/)
- **Summary**: 现有一次性深度剪枝方法依赖局部层重要性或固定冗余假设。论文提出局部感知冗余剪枝（LoRP），利用表示局部性分数（RLS）对层进行聚类并分配剪枝。实验表明LoRP在多种LLM家族上改善了困惑度和下游任务准确率。

<h3 id="672">[672] Semantic Flow Regularization: Teaching LLMs to Generate Diverse Yet Coherent Responses</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27971](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27971) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kerui Peng, Feifei Li, Xingyu Fan, Wenhui Que
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text Generation, Flow Matching, Controllable Generation
- **Summary**: 条件微调导致LLM输出多样性严重受限，称为跨风格坍缩。论文提出语义流正则化（SFR），用条件流匹配在连续句嵌入上监督骨干，推理时丢弃额外头。在工业对话数据集上提升多样性、风格保真度和质量，在LiveCodeBench上提升pass@k，且多token预测是SFR的退化特例。

<h3 id="673">[673] KVoiceBench, KOpenAudioBench, and KMMAU: Agent-Driven Korean Speech Benchmarks for Evaluating SpeechLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.27984](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27984) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haechan Kim, Seungjun Chung, Inkyu Park, Jihoo Lee, Jonghyun Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speech Evaluation, Multilingual Learning, Spoken Language Model
- **Summary**: 现有语音语言模型评估集中于英语，直接迁移会破坏目标语言特性。提出两种人工-代理基准构建框架，构建了三个韩语语音基准（共12,345样本）。评估8个模型发现英韩性能差距因模型和任务而异，SpokenQA与音频理解排名不一致，暴露了仅英语评估无法发现的互补缺陷。

<h3 id="674">[674] Integrated and Cross-Architecture Interpretation of LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28006](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28006) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Leonardo Matthew Yauw, Wei-Bin Kou, Yujiu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LeonardoMatthew/IAR)
- **Summary**: 单一探针（如MIP或DTR）难以全面理解LLM推理结构。提出集成跨架构推理解释框架IAR，结合带宽校准MIP、Tukey IQR峰值检测、跨层轨迹分析和Jaccard稳定性验证。在六个模型、四个领域上验证了其泛化性。

<h3 id="675">[675] Extracting Small Translation Specialists from LLMs by Aggressively Pruning Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28042) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Liu O. Martin, Lucas Bandarkar, Nanyun Peng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Mixture-of-Experts, Machine Translation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型对机器翻译任务过度参数化。提出利用MoE专家专业化特性，激进剪除与翻译无关的专家。无需训练可剪除50%专家，微调后可剪除75%甚至近90%专家，同时保持翻译质量。

<h3 id="676">[676] BenGER: Benchmarking LLM Systems on Subsumption-Based Legal Reasoning in German Law</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28183) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sebastian Nagl, Ann-Kristin Mayrhofer, Martin Heidebach, Aleyna Koçak, Anne Zettelmeier, Elly Breu, Angelina Greiner, Sofija Milijas, Matthias Grabmair
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 针对德国法中的包容性法律推理缺乏评估基准。构建BenGER基准，包含596个考试型任务和531个教义推理任务。评估12个LLM，闭源旗舰领先，人机共创优于人类单独，LLM评委与人类评分Pearson r=0.76。

<h3 id="677">[677] Reverse Probing: Supervised Token-level Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Models in Clinical Text</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28740) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bushi Xiao, Sarvesh Soni, Daisy Zhe Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Clinical NLP, Summarization
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 现有不确定性量化无法在长临床文本中定位token级不确定性。提出Reverse Probing框架，直接利用已有标注摘要从内部激活中提取不确定性信号。在两个临床数据集上超过8个基线，AUPRC最高提升4倍，降低推理成本。

<h3 id="678">[678] Skill-Conditioned Gated Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.28791](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28791) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiazhen Huang, Xiao Chen, Xiao Luo, Yong Dai, Senkang Hu, Yuzhi Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Distillation, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Findings
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/walawalagoose/SGSD)
- **Summary**: 现有on-policy自蒸馏假设特权信息可信，而技能库可能不可靠。提出SGSD，将技能自蒸馏视为教师假设验证，通过多教师池和门控目标蒸馏信息性信号。在数学推理基准上，Qwen3-1.7B平均超过GRPO 6.2%，超过OPSD 1.7%。

<h3 id="679">[679] OISD: On-Policy Internal Self-Distillation of Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29089](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29089) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Liu, Darryl Cherian Jacob, Yang Zhou, Jindong Wang, Pan He
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Distillation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/THE-MALT-LAB/OISD)
- **Summary**: RL后训练主要优化最终输出，忽略中间表示中的预测信号。提出OISD框架，将最终层的on-policy预测信号通过logit和attention对齐迁移到中间层。在四个数学推理任务上相较于强RL基线取得持续提升。

<h3 id="680">[680] Relevance as a Vulnerability: How Web Retrieval Degrades Safety Alignment in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29224](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29224) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aditya Nawal, Manit Baser, Mohan Gurusamy
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, RAG, LLM Safety
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Findings
- **Summary**: 检索增强智能体会削弱安全对齐。提出AgentREVEAL诊断框架，发现“安全来源悖论”：即使安全警告页面也使有害合规平均增加25%。相关性是共同触发条件，并构建HarmURLBench基准。

<h3 id="681">[681] How Coding Agents Fail Their Users: A Large-Scale Analysis of Developer-Agent Misalignment in 20,574 Real-World Sessions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29442) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ningzhi Tang, Chaoran Chen, Gelei Xu, Yiyu Shi, Yu Huang, Collin McMillan, Tao Dong, Toby Jia-Jun Li
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Debugging, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 现有编码智能体失败分析依赖基准轨迹，无法反映真实开发者体验。对20,574个会话进行观察研究，识别七种失准形式。90.50%造成努力与信任成本，91.49%需要用户显式纠正，IDE与CLI模式存在差异。

<h3 id="682">[682] LaRA: Layer-wise Representation Analysis for Detecting Data Contamination in RL Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29888) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Minju Gwak, Minseo Kwak, Dongseok Lee, Guijin Son, Alan Ritter, Jaehyung Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Contamination, Representation Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: RL后训练中的数据污染检测方法缺乏研究，且输出级信号不可靠。提出LaRA层表示分析框架，使用三个互补指标检测污染。实验表明其优于现有输出级基线。

<h3 id="683">[683] Exploring Autonomous Agentic Data Engineering for Model Specialization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30407](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30407) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yujie Luo, Xiangyuan Ru, Jingsheng Zheng, Jingjing Wang, Yuqi Zhu, Jintian Zhang, Runnan Fang, Kewei Xu, Ye Liu, Zheng Wei, Jiang Bian, Zang Li, Shumin Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Data Curation, Data Synthesis
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026 Main Conference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zjunlp/DataAgent)
- **Summary**: 现有LLM数据整理依赖人工流程。形式化自主智能体数据工程任务，让智能体自主规划、生成和迭代优化训练数据。GPT-5.2通过数据适应使student模型提升57.29%。

<h3 id="684">[684] FBHM: Functional Benchmarking and Steering of VLMs for Hateful Meme Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31349) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Paramananda Bhaskar, Naquee Rizwan, Daksh Jogchand, Saurabh Kumar Pandey, Animesh Mukherjee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Hate Speech Detection, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有仇恨梗图基准是观察性的，混淆修辞机制与目标社区特征。构建FBHM基准（25种功能×10个社区，共5000条），并提出LSV学习转向向量。LSV在500个样本上将FBHM性能提升约30个Macro-F1点。

<h3 id="685">[685] Vision-Language Models Suppress Female Representations Under Ambiguous Input</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.31556](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.31556) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Arnau Marin-Llobet, Simon Henniger, Mahzarin R. Banaji
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Gender Bias, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Venue**: EMNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 视觉语言模型在模糊输入下的性别偏差研究不足。提出LALS指标，通过将视觉token激活投影到文本嵌入空间来测量概念关联。发现模型内部编码女性关联但输出男性，层分析显示不对称过滤。

<h3 id="686">[686] Skill or Skip? Learning Selective Skill Invocation in Agentic Tasks via Dual-Granularity Preference Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00510) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chishui Chen, Jiaye Lin, Te Sun, Yi Yang, Junxi Wang, Cong Qin, Yangen Hu, Lu Pan, Ke Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Preference Optimization
- **Summary**: 现有方法忽略技能是否应在当前决策点被调用。提出SelSkill双粒度偏好学习框架，将技能使用视为技能或跳过决策。在ALFWorld上任务成功率比技能基线高10.9，BFCL上高5.7。

<h3 id="687">[687] Linguistics-Aware Non-Distortionary LLM Watermarking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00613) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shinwoo Park, Hyejin Park, Hyeseon An, Yo-Sub Han
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Watermarking, Multilingual Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Shinwoo-Park/luna_watermark)
- **Summary**: 水印需要在多语言部署中不降质且可验证。提出LUNA，语言自适应水印，结合无模型检测和单token非失真。在12个设置中AUROC达0.9959，中位困惑度偏移0.045，9/12设置达到AUROC\>0.99且偏移\<0.1。

<h3 id="688">[688] DASH: Dual-Branch Score Distillation for Guidance-Calibrated Compact Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00798](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00798) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Abdullah Al Shafi, Kazi Saeed Alam, Sk Imran Hossain, Engelbert Mephu Nguifo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Knowledge Distillation, Model Compression
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/C-loud-Nine/DASH_Dual-Branch-Score-Distillation)
- **Summary**: 类条件扩散模型的输出级蒸馏无法识别两个分数分支，导致指导失效。提出DASH，独立监督条件和无条件分支，并加入锚点和教师课程。超过5倍压缩学生模型距教师FID在4点内，恢复至少89%指导差距。

<h3 id="689">[689] The Value of Spike Timing: A Leakage-Resistant Benchmark of SNN Design Choices for Network Intrusion Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01442) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Raj Patel, Shaswata Mitra, David Amebley, Taye Akinrele, Sayanton Dibbo, Shahram Rahimi
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network, Intrusion Detection System
- **Summary**: 针对SNN用于网络入侵检测时神经元模型与脉冲编码缺乏系统性对比、评估协议可能泄漏信息的问题，作者在4个基准上评估了9种神经元的27种配置，并使用仅训练集变换、种子独立划分等严格协议复查。结果表明LeakyParallel/latency在两种27配置评估中均排第一，筛选顺序在确认下基本一致；在T=25处延迟编码在所有5个确认协议上提升macro-F1，且延迟编码效果来自时间信息与网络动态交互。稀疏输入编码并不直接带来内部稀疏活动，延迟编码的fanout加权操作少于速率编码。

<h3 id="690">[690] FSA-GRPO: Teaching Auditory LLMs to Use Few-Shot Demonstrations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02615](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02615) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haolong Zheng, Siyin Wang, Xulin Fan, Zengrui Jin, Mark Hasegawa-Johnson
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Audio-Language Model, In-Context Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对听觉大语言模型未专门训练成演示条件推理格式，限制少样本ICL效果的问题，提出FSA-GRPO，一种基于RL的后训练方案，用专门奖励鼓励模型利用少样本演示。仅用2k高资源成人ASR话语训练即可提升通用少样本适应能力，儿童语音识别相对WER降低53.9%，还改善多语言ASR、语音翻译和音频理解；无领域数据时比直接在外域数据上微调更有效。

<h3 id="691">[691] GRZO: Group-Relative Zeroth-Order Optimization for Large Language Model Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02857](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02857) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Liyan Tan, Yequan Zhao, Yifan Yang, Ruijie Zhang, Xinling Yu, Zheng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Zeroth-Order Optimization, Fine-Tuning, Memory-Efficient Training
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2026
